### 5.5 Cross-Internal Validation — Layout 5C

| Check | Rule | Blocking |
|---|---|---|
| Certificate ID consistency | Consistent across all certificate sections | HARD FAIL |
| Multiple certificates — unique IDs | Each additional NSDC certificate must have a different Certificate ID | HARD FAIL |
| Job role consistency | If multiple certificates uploaded, job roles should be related (not contradictory) | Soft flag |

---

### 5.6 Global Validation — Layout 5C

| Check | Rule | Blocking |
|---|---|---|
| Work order payment in bank credits | Work value ±30%, completion date ±7 days in Step-3 canonical CSV | Soft flag |
| Candidate name vs Step-2 Aadhaar name | Confirmed at global level | HARD FAIL |
| Training institute jurisdiction vs Step-1 address | State-level plausibility check | Soft flag |

Rendered when `verified_profile.work_type = "freelancer"`.

No server-side API. Validated entirely via OCR + Step-2 global identity + bank transaction match.

| 4–7 | Client Invoice Samples 2–5 | JPG / PNG / PDF | Optional |
| 8 | Portfolio / Project Completion Proof | JPG / PNG / PDF | Optional |
| 9 | GST Registration Certificate | PDF | Optional |

### 6.2 OCR Extracts — Layout 5D

**From Platform Profile Screenshot:**

| Field | Example | Used For |
|---|---|---|
| Account Holder Name | Ravi Kumar | Step-1 name match |
| Platform Rating | 4.8 / 5.0 | P5 ML feature |
| Total Lifetime Earnings (₹) | $12,450 | Earnings scale reference |
| Member Since Date | March 2022 | Tenure calculation |
| Badge / Level | Top Rated | P5 ML feature |

**From Client Invoice:**

| Field | Example | Used For |
|---|---|---|
| Client Name | TechFlow Pvt Ltd | Client verification |
| Service Description | UI/UX Design | Occupation match |
| Invoice Amount (₹ or $) | ₹35,000 | Bank credit cross-match |
| Invoice Date | 05-11-2025 | Bank credit date match |
| Payment Due Date | 15-11-2025 | Payment timing check |
| Payment Status | Paid | Completed work proof |
| GST Number (if applicable) | 33AAAAA0000A1Z5 | Optional identity anchor |

---

### 6.3 Individual Validation — Layout 5D

| Check | Rule | Blocking |
|---|---|---|
| Platform account name (OCR) vs Step-1 name | Fuzzy ≥ 85% | HARD FAIL |
| Invoice amount | Must be > 0 | HARD FAIL |
| Invoice date | Must be a valid date | HARD FAIL |
| Payment status | Must be "Paid" or equivalent | Soft flag |
| All screenshots from same account | Account name consistent across all screenshots | HARD FAIL |

**Error Messages:**
- "Platform account name does not match your registered name."
- "Invoice amount is invalid."

---

### 6.4 Cross-Internal Validation — Layout 5D

| Check | Rule | Blocking |
|---|---|---|
| Same platform account across all screenshots | Account name consistent | HARD FAIL |
| Invoice dates not all identical | If all invoices share exact same date → fraud flag | HARD FAIL |
| Platform account name across all invoices | Consistent service provider name | HARD FAIL |

**Error Messages:**
- "All invoices appear to have the same date. Please upload genuine client invoices."

---

### 6.5 Global Validation — Layout 5D

| Check | Rule | Blocking |
|---|---|---|
| Invoice payments in bank credits | Invoice amount ±30%, invoice date ±7 days in Step-3 canonical CSV | Soft flag |
| Platform account name vs Step-1 name | Confirmed at global level | HARD FAIL |
| Foreign currency conversion (if applicable) | Invoice in USD/GBP converted at approximate rate for bank match | Soft flag |

Triggered when ALL mandatory fields for the selected layout pass all validation layers, or when the user explicitly skips Step-5.

```json
// Example: Layout 5A (Platform Worker)
verified_profile.work = {

  "insurance_verified"      : true,
  "insurance_expiry"        : "2026-07-31",
  "vehicle_owner_mismatch"  : false,
  "platform_name"           : "Swiggy",
  "platform_tenure_months"  : 18,
  "platform_rating"         : 4.3,
  "trips_per_month"         : 142,
  "platform_income_verified": true,
  "monthly_earnings_inr"    : 18450,

}
```

```json
// Example: Layout 5B (Vendor / Seller)
verified_profile.work = {
```

## Section 8 — ML Features Produced

### Layout 5A — Platform Worker

| Feature | Description | Pillar |
|---|---|---|
| `dl_valid_binary` | DL is active and not expired | P5 |
| `rc_active_binary` | RC is active | P5 |
| `insurance_active_binary` | Vehicle insurance active | P5 |
| `platform_tenure_months` | Months on platform since join date | P1 |
| `platform_earnings_norm` | Monthly earnings normalized | P1 |
| `trips_per_month` | Delivery / trip count last 3 months avg | P1 |
| `bank_to_platform_ratio` | Bank credit vs platform stated earnings | P1 |
| `platform_income_verified` | Bank credit matched to screenshot (binary) | P1 |
| `platform_rating_norm` | Rating score normalized 0–1 | P5 |
| `vehicle_owner_mismatch` | RC owner ≠ Aadhaar name (binary) | P5 |

### Layout 5B — Vendor / Seller

| Feature | Description | Pillar |
|---|---|---|
| `svanidhi_verified_binary` | SVANidhi loan disbursed | P3 |
| `svanidhi_loan_norm` | Loan amount normalized | P3 |
| `next_loan_eligible_norm` | Upgrade eligibility normalized | P3 |
| `trade_licence_verified_binary` | Trade licence valid | P5 |
| `svanidhi_repayment_rate` | Monthly repayments found in bank CSV | P1 |
| `vendor_tenure_norm` | Estimated vendor tenure from approval date | P1 |

### Layout 5C — Skilled Tradesperson

| Feature | Description | Pillar |
|---|---|---|
| `nsdc_certified_binary` | Certificate valid | P5 |
| `nsqf_level_norm` | NSQF level normalized (1–8 → 0–1) | P5 |
| `work_order_verified_binary` | Work order document provided | P1 |
| `work_order_value_norm` | Work order amount normalized | P1 |
| `multi_skill_bonus` | > 1 NSDC certificate (binary) | P5 |
| `work_income_bank_match` | Work order value matched in bank | P1 |

### Layout 5D — Freelancer

| Feature | Description | Pillar |
|---|---|---|
| `client_invoice_verified_binary` | At least 1 invoice matched in bank | P1 |
| `invoice_total_norm` | Total invoiced amount normalized | P1 |
| `invoice_count` | Number of client invoices uploaded | P1 |
| `platform_rating_norm` | Freelance rating normalized 0–1 | P5 |
| `freelance_tenure_months` | Months on platform since join | P1 |
| `multi_platform_bonus` | Active on > 1 platform (binary) | P5 |

---

## Section 9 — Technical Flow (Final)

```
User opens Step-5
  ↓
System reads verified_profile.work_type from Step-1
  ↓
Renders correct layout (5A / 5B / 5C / 5D)
  ↓
[SKIP PATH]
User taps "Skip this step"
  → step5_skipped = true
  → verified_profile.work = null
  → Step-5 Status = SKIPPED
  → Continue button enabled
  → Navigate to Step-6
  ↓
[SUBMIT PATH]
--- LAYOUT 5A ---
User enters vehicle registration number
  → POST /api/gov/vehicle-rc-check
  [Fail] → Show error, uploads locked
  [Pass] → rc_api_verified = true
  → POST /api/gov/vehicle-insurance-check (auto-triggered)
  [Fail] → Show error, uploads locked
  [Pass] → insurance_api_verified = true
  ↓
*** GATE 1 OPENS — all upload slots enabled ***
  ↓
User uploads RC Book, DL Front, DL Back, Insurance Certificate
  → Per-document OCR extraction
  → Individual validation for each document
  → Cross-check between documents (vehicle number, owner name, DL class)
  ↓
User uploads 3 platform earning screenshots
  → Per-screenshot OCR: earnings, trips, rating, account name, tenure
  → Account name vs Step-1 name (HARD FAIL if mismatch)
  → Optional 2nd and 3rd platform screenshots processed if uploaded
  ↓
Cross-internal validation:
  → Vehicle number consistent across RC, insurance, screenshots
  → All 3 screenshots from same platform account
  ↓
Global validation:
  → Platform earnings vs Step-3 bank credits (±30%, ±7 days)
  → DL name confirmed vs Step-2 Aadhaar name (global level)
  ↓
All pass → work_verified = true for 5A

--- LAYOUT 5B ---
User enters SVANidhi Application ID
  → POST /api/gov/svanidhi-verify
  [Fail] → Show error, uploads locked
  [Pass] → svanidhi_api_verified = true
  ↓
*** GATE 1 OPENS ***
User uploads SVANidhi Letter, Trade Licence
  → OCR extraction + individual validation
  → Beneficiary name vs API vs Aadhaar name
  → Trade licence holder name vs Aadhaar name
  → Optional: No-Due Certificate, Association, Market Letter, GST
  ↓
Cross-internal + Global validation
All pass → work_verified = true for 5B

--- LAYOUT 5C ---
User enters Skill Certificate ID
  → POST /api/gov/skill-verify
  [Fail] → Show error, uploads locked
  [Pass] → skill_api_verified = true
  ↓
*** GATE 1 OPENS ***
User uploads NSDC Certificate, Work Order Letter
  → OCR extraction + individual validation
  → Candidate name vs API vs Aadhaar name
  → NSQF level vs API response
  → Optional: Experience cert, additional NSDC certs, GST
  ↓
Cross-internal + Global validation
All pass → work_verified = true for 5C

--- LAYOUT 5D ---
All upload slots available immediately (no API gate)
  ↓
User uploads Platform Profile Screenshot + Client Invoice(s)
  → OCR: account name, rating, tenure, earnings (from screenshot)
  → OCR: client, amount, date, status (from invoices)
  → Platform account name vs Step-1 name (HARD FAIL if mismatch)
  → Invoice date uniqueness check (HARD FAIL if all identical)
  → Optional: 2nd platform, portfolio, GST
  ↓
Cross-internal + Global validation:
  → Invoice amounts vs Step-3 bank credits (±30%, ±7 days)
All pass → work_verified = true for 5D

--- COMMON FINAL PATH ---
  ↓
All HARD FAILs cleared
All soft flags recorded
  ↓
*** GATE 2 OPENS — Continue button enabled ***
  ↓
Update verified_profile.work with all extracted data
  ↓
Mark Step-5 Status = VERIFIED
  ↓
Enable navigation to Step-6
```

---

## Section 10 — Output of Step-5

| Output | Value |
|---|---|
| Step-5 Status | `VERIFIED` or `SKIPPED` |
| `verified_profile.work` | Populated per layout (or null if skipped) |
| `step5_skipped` | `true` if user skipped, `false` if submitted |
| P1 ML features | Income stability features extracted |
| P5 ML features | Work identity features extracted |
| Step-6 Status | `UNLOCKED` |

### Cross-Step Dependencies Resolved by Step-5

- **Step-9 (Scoring):** All 5A/5B/5C/5D ML features used directly in P1 and P5 pillar computation
- **Step-7 (Insurance):** `vehicle_number`, `insurance_expiry`, `rc_verified` → used to pre-fill vehicle insurance validation in Step-7

---

*GigCredit Validation & Verification Specification — Step-5 Work Proof Verification*
*Version 1.0 | April 2026*

--- specification folders_new\input vaidation and verification\step wise input validation\STEP-6-GOVERNMENT-SCHEME-VERIFICATION-UPDATED.txt ---
SYSTEM DESIGN PROMPT — STEP-6: GOVERNMENT SCHEME VERIFICATION
GigCredit Onboarding | Version: Final (Updated) | Pillar: P7 Social Accountability
===========================================================================

PURPOSE
-------
Step-6 collects optional government scheme registrations and financial
security programs associated with the user. These schemes strengthen
financial credibility and improve scoring signals under the P7 (Social
Accountability) pillar and cross-feed P2 (Payment Discipline) and
P3 (Debt Management).

All schemes in this step are OPTIONAL globally.
If a user selects any scheme, that scheme's ID and proof upload become
CONDITIONALLY REQUIRED. Invalid inputs are rejected and ignored.

Supported Schemes (6 total):
  Scheme 1 : eShram Worker Registration
  Scheme 2 : PM-SYM (Pradhan Mantri Shram Yogi Maandhan)
  Scheme 3 : PMJJBY (Pradhan Mantri Jeevan Jyoti Bima Yojana)
  Scheme 4 : PMMY (Pradhan Mantri Mudra Yojana)
  Scheme 5 : PPF (Public Provident Fund)
  Scheme 6 : Udyam / MSME Registration  ← NEW (previously missing)

Validation architecture:
  - Individual Validation : Per-field rules on user-entered data + OCR
  - API Verification      : Government simulation (FastAPI + MongoDB)
  - Bank Cross-Check      : Scheme payments matched against Step-3 CSV
  - Cross-Internal        : Consistency within scheme documents
  - Global Validation     : Identity cross-check with Step-1, Step-2, Step-3


===========================================================================
HOW IT IS PRESENTED ON SCREEN
===========================================================================

A checklist screen showing 6 scheme cards.
Each card shows:
  - Scheme name and short description
  - Estimated score impact
  - Expand button to reveal input fields

Unselected schemes remain collapsed and are skipped entirely.
The entire step is optional — user may skip all schemes.

Step-level message shown to user:
  "Each government scheme you are enrolled in adds to your
   Social Accountability score. Even 1–2 verified schemes
   can improve your final credit score by 15 to 30 points."


===========================================================================
SCHEME 1 — ESHRAM WORKER REGISTRATION
===========================================================================

Score impact: "Can add up to 12 points"

INPUTS:
  Input 1 : UAN Number (Conditional Required when scheme selected)
            Format  : 12-digit numeric
            Example : 123456789012
  Input 2 : eShram Card Upload (Conditional Required)
            Format  : JPG or PNG

UI BEHAVIOUR:
  UAN number entry → User clicks Verify
  If API succeeds → eShram Card upload enabled
  If API fails    → Error shown, scheme marked unverified


API VERIFICATION — ESHRAM
  Endpoint  : POST /gov/eshram/verify
  MongoDB   : eshram_db
  Request   : { "uan": "123456789012" }
  Response  : {
                "worker_name"          : "Ravi Kumar",
                "occupation"           : "Electrician",
                "state"                : "Tamil Nadu",
                "registration_status"  : "Active",
                "registration_date"    : "2022-06-15",
                "verified"             : true
              }
  Possible status: Active | Inactive | Not Found


OCR EXTRACTION — ESHRAM CARD
  Fields extracted:
    worker_name         : Name of registered worker
    fathers_name        : Father's name
    date_of_birth       : DOB of worker
    gender              : Male / Female / Other
    uan_number          : 12-digit UAN
    primary_occupation  : Work category
    aadhaar_reference   : Masked Aadhaar (last 4 digits)
    photo               : Worker photo on card


INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — ESHRAM
  Field               | Rule                                          | Blocking
  --------------------|-----------------------------------------------|---------
  UAN Number          | Must be exactly 12 digits                     | HARD FAIL
                      | Numeric only                                  |
                      | Must not be all zeros                         |
  API Status          | registration_status must be Active            | HARD FAIL
  OCR Worker Name     | Must match API worker_name (fuzzy ≥ 85%)      | HARD FAIL
  OCR UAN Number      | Must match user-entered UAN exactly           | HARD FAIL
  OCR DOB             | Must be a valid date                          | Soft flag
  Aadhaar Reference   | Must show last 4 digits (XXXX-XXXX-ABCD)      | Soft flag


CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — ESHRAM
  Check                                               | Blocking
  ----------------------------------------------------|----------
  UAN on card must match UAN entered by user          | HARD FAIL
  Worker name consistent across front and back of card| Soft flag


GLOBAL VALIDATION — ESHRAM
  Field               | Compared Against                | Blocking
  --------------------|----------------------------------|---------
  Worker Name         | Step-1 Full Name (fuzzy ≥ 85%)  | Soft flag
  Date of Birth       | Step-2 Aadhaar DOB (exact)      | Soft flag
  State               | Step-1 State of Residence       | Soft flag

Global profile update:
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.eshram = true
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.eshram_occupation = "Electrician"


===========================================================================
SCHEME 2 — PM-SYM (PRADHAN MANTRI SHRAM YOGI MAANDHAN)
===========================================================================

Score impact: "Can add up to 10 points"

INPUTS:
  Input 1 : Pension Account Number (Conditional Required)
            Format  : Alphanumeric (e.g. PM-SYM-829201)
  Input 2 : Pension Card or Registration Certificate (Conditional Required)
            Format  : JPG, PNG, or PDF


API VERIFICATION — PM-SYM
  Endpoint  : GET /api/pension/status
  MongoDB   : pmsym_db
  Request   : {
                "aadhaar_number"         : "XXXX-XXXX-1234",
                "pension_account_number" : "PM-SYM-829201"
              }
  Response  : {
                "scheme"                 : "PM-SYM",
                "status"                 : "ACTIVE",
                "monthly_contribution"   : 100,
                "last_payment_date"      : "2026-02-10",
                "pension_account_number" : "PM-SYM-829201",
                "verified"               : true
              }
  Possible status: ACTIVE | INACTIVE | NOT_FOUND


OCR EXTRACTION — PM-SYM CARD
  Fields extracted:
    pension_account_number  : Unique subscriber ID
    subscriber_name         : Name on card
    date_of_birth           : DOB
    gender                  : Male / Female / Other
    pension_start_date      : Date enrolled
    monthly_contribution    : Amount (₹55 / ₹100 / ₹200)
    government_contribution : Matching govt amount
    aadhaar_reference       : Masked Aadhaar


INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — PM-SYM
  Field                    | Rule                                      | Blocking
  -------------------------|-------------------------------------------|---------
  Pension Account Number   | Must match PM-SYM-XXXXXX format           | HARD FAIL
  API Status               | Must be ACTIVE                            | HARD FAIL
  OCR Account Number       | Must match entered account number         | HARD FAIL
  OCR Subscriber Name      | Must match API name (fuzzy ≥ 85%)         | HARD FAIL
  Monthly Contribution     | Must be ₹55, ₹100, or ₹200 (valid tiers) | Soft flag


CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — PM-SYM
  Check                                                  | Blocking
  -------------------------------------------------------|---------
  Pension account number consistent across card sections | HARD FAIL
  Contribution amount must align with age-based tier     | Soft flag


BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — PM-SYM
  Rule: Step-3 canonical CSV must show monthly debit
        matching contribution amount (± ₹5 tolerance)
  Date gap between consecutive debits must be ≤ 35 days
  If bank match found → pmsym_bank_verified = true (strong P2 signal)
  If no match        → Soft flag; scheme accepted as declared


GLOBAL VALIDATION — PM-SYM
  Field             | Compared Against               | Blocking
  ------------------|--------------------------------|---------
  Subscriber Name   | Step-1 Full Name (fuzzy ≥ 85%) | Soft flag
  DOB               | Step-2 Aadhaar DOB (exact)     | Soft flag

Global profile update:
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.pmsym = true
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.pmsym_contribution = 100


===========================================================================
SCHEME 3 — PMJJBY (PRADHAN MANTRI JEEVAN JYOTI BIMA YOJANA)
===========================================================================

Score impact: "Can add up to 8 points"
Note: No verification API for PMJJBY. Validation via OCR + bank cross-check.

INPUTS:
  Input 1 : PMJJBY Insurance Certificate Upload (Conditional Required)
            Format  : JPG, PNG, or PDF


OCR EXTRACTION — PMJJBY CERTIFICATE
  Fields extracted:
    member_name           : Insured person's name
    master_policy_number  : Bank-issued master policy number
    urn                   : Unique Reference Number
    date_of_birth         : DOB of insured
    bank_account_number   : Linked savings account
    bank_name             : Issuing bank
    insurance_provider    : LIC or partner insurer
    date_of_commencement  : Policy start date
    cover_end_date        : Policy expiry date (31 May each year)
    sum_assured           : ₹2,00,000 (fixed)
    premium_amount_paid   : ₹436 per year (fixed)
    renewal_date          : Annual renewal date


INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — PMJJBY
  Field                 | Rule                                        | Blocking
  ----------------------|---------------------------------------------|---------
  Member Name           | Must be readable; min 3 chars               | HARD FAIL
  Master Policy Number  | Must be readable and non-empty              | HARD FAIL
  URN                   | Must be readable and non-empty              | HARD FAIL
  Sum Assured           | Must be ₹2,00,000 (fixed — any other = fake)| HARD FAIL
  Premium Amount        | Must be ₹436 (fixed — any other = flag)     | Soft flag
  Cover End Date        | Must be 31st May of a valid year            | Soft flag
  DOB                   | Must be a valid date                        | Soft flag


CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — PMJJBY
  Check                                              | Blocking
  ---------------------------------------------------|----------
  Policy start date must be before cover end date    | HARD FAIL
  Bank account number on cert must be readable       | Soft flag


BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — PMJJBY
  Rule: Step-3 CSV must show annual debit of ₹436
        on or near policy renewal date (± 15 days)
  Merchant narration should contain: LIC / PMJJBY / JEEVAN JYOTI
  If bank match found → pmjjby_bank_verified = true


GLOBAL VALIDATION — PMJJBY
  Field               | Compared Against               | Blocking
  --------------------|--------------------------------|---------
  Member Name         | Step-1 Full Name (fuzzy ≥ 85%) | Soft flag
  Bank Account Number | Step-3 Verified Account Number | Soft flag

Global profile update:
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.pmjjby = true


===========================================================================
SCHEME 4 — PMMY (PRADHAN MANTRI MUDRA YOJANA)
===========================================================================

Score impact: "Can add up to 10 points"
Note: No verification API. Validation via OCR + bank cross-check.

INPUTS:
  Input 1 : Loan Account Number (Conditional Required)
            Format  : Alphanumeric
  Input 2 : Mudra Loan Acknowledgement / Sanction Letter (Conditional Required)
            Format  : JPG, PNG, or PDF
  Input 3 : Bank No-Due Certificate after loan closure (Optional)
            Format  : JPG, PNG, or PDF


OCR EXTRACTION — MUDRA LOAN LETTER
  Fields extracted:
    applicant_name     : Borrower name
    loan_amount        : Sanctioned amount (₹)
    loan_account_number: Unique loan account ID
    bank_name          : Lending institution
    loan_approval_date : Date loan was approved
    loan_type          : Shishu / Kishor / Tarun
    mudra_card_number  : 16-digit Mudra card (if issued)

OCR EXTRACTION — NO-DUE CERTIFICATE (if uploaded)
  Fields extracted:
    loan_account_number : Must match Mudra letter
    borrower_name       : Must match Mudra letter
    certificate_date    : Date of no-due certificate
    issuing_bank        : Must match Mudra letter bank
    loan_status         : Closed / Settled


INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — PMMY
  Field               | Rule                                        | Blocking
  --------------------|---------------------------------------------|---------
  Loan Account Number | Alphanumeric, min 6 characters              | HARD FAIL
  Applicant Name      | Readable, min 3 chars                       | HARD FAIL
  Loan Amount         | Must be > 0 and ≤ ₹10,00,000 (Mudra limit) | HARD FAIL
  Loan Type           | Must be Shishu / Kishor / Tarun             | Soft flag
  Approval Date       | Must be a valid past date                   | Soft flag


CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — PMMY
  Field                | Mudra Letter     | No-Due Certificate | Blocking
  ---------------------|------------------|--------------------|----------
  Loan Account Number  | Must match       | Must match         | HARD FAIL
  Applicant Name       | Source           | Must match         | HARD FAIL
  Bank Name            | Source           | Must match         | Soft flag


BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — PMMY
  Rule 1: Loan disbursement credit in Step-3 CSV
          Amount: Mudra loan amount ± 10%
          Date  : Near loan_approval_date ± 15 days
  Rule 2: Monthly EMI repayments found as recurring debits
          Merchant: bank name / MUDRA / LOAN / EMI in narration
  If disbursement found → mudra_credited = true
  If EMIs found        → mudra_repaying = true (strong P3 positive)
  If no-due cert uploaded + loan status = Closed → mudra_closed = true


GLOBAL VALIDATION — PMMY
  Field           | Compared Against               | Blocking
  ----------------|--------------------------------|---------
  Applicant Name  | Step-1 Full Name (fuzzy ≥ 85%) | Soft flag
  Bank Name       | Step-3 Verified Bank Name      | Soft flag

Global profile update:
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.pmmy = true
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.mudra_loan_amount = 50000
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.mudra_closed = false


===========================================================================
SCHEME 5 — PPF (PUBLIC PROVIDENT FUND)
===========================================================================

Score impact: "Can add up to 8 points"
Note: No verification API. Validation via OCR + bank cross-check.

INPUTS:
  Input 1 : PPF Account Number (Conditional Required)
            Format  : Alphanumeric
  Input 2 : PPF Passbook First Page (Conditional Required)
            Format  : JPG or PNG


OCR EXTRACTION — PPF PASSBOOK
  Fields extracted:
    account_holder_name : Name on passbook
    ppf_account_number  : Unique PPF account ID
    cif_number          : Customer Information File number
    bank_name           : Holding bank
    branch_name         : Branch name
    date_of_issue       : Passbook issue date
    ifsc_code           : Branch IFSC
    annual_contribution : Typical deposit amount (₹)
    maturity_date       : 15-year lock-in end date


INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — PPF
  Field                | Rule                                          | Blocking
  ---------------------|-----------------------------------------------|---------
  PPF Account Number   | Alphanumeric, min 6 characters               | HARD FAIL
  OCR Account Number   | Must match entered account number            | HARD FAIL
  Account Holder Name  | Readable, min 3 chars                        | HARD FAIL
  Annual Contribution  | Must be ≥ ₹500 and ≤ ₹1,50,000 (PPF limits) | Soft flag
  Maturity Date        | Must be a valid future or past date          | Soft flag


CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — PPF
  Check                                                | Blocking
  -----------------------------------------------------|----------
  PPF account number consistent across passbook pages  | HARD FAIL
  IFSC code format valid (11 characters)               | Soft flag


BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — PPF
  Rule: Step-3 CSV must show quarterly or annual debit
        Amount: annual_contribution amount ± 10%
        Merchant narration: PPF / PUBLIC PROVIDENT / SBI PPF / HDFC PPF
  If bank match found → ppf_bank_verified = true (P4 savings signal)


GLOBAL VALIDATION — PPF
  Field                | Compared Against                  | Blocking
  ---------------------|-----------------------------------|---------
  Account Holder Name  | Step-1 Full Name (fuzzy ≥ 85%)    | Soft flag
  Bank Name            | Step-3 Verified Bank Name         | Soft flag
  IFSC Code            | Step-3 Verified IFSC (if same bank)| Soft flag

Global profile update:
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.ppf = true
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.ppf_contribution = 12000


===========================================================================
SCHEME 6 — UDYAM / MSME REGISTRATION  ← NEWLY ADDED
===========================================================================

Score impact: "Can add up to 15 points"
Note: Strongest formal business signal — significantly boosts P7 for
      Vendor, Freelancer, and Skilled Tradesperson work types.

INPUTS:
  Input 1 : Udyam Registration Number (Conditional Required)
            Format  : UDYAM-XX-00-0000000 (19-character format)
            Example : UDYAM-TN-33-0012345
  Input 2 : Udyam Registration Certificate Upload (Conditional Required)
            Format  : PDF preferred; JPG or PNG accepted

UI BEHAVIOUR:
  User enters Udyam Registration Number → clicks Verify
  If API succeeds → Certificate upload becomes enabled
  If API fails    → Error shown, scheme marked unverified


API VERIFICATION — UDYAM / MSME
  Endpoint  : POST /gov/msme/udyam-verify
  MongoDB   : udyam_db
  Request   : { "udyam_number": "UDYAM-TN-33-0012345" }
  Response  : {
                "enterprise_name"      : "Ravi Kumar Enterprises",
                "udyam_number"         : "UDYAM-TN-33-0012345",
                "category"             : "Micro",
                "nic_activity"         : "Retail Trade",
                "registration_date"    : "2021-08-15",
                "state"                : "Tamil Nadu",
                "status"               : "Active",
                "major_activity"       : "Services",
                "verified"             : true
              }
  Possible status: Active | Cancelled | Suspended | Not Found
  Possible category: Micro | Small | Medium


OCR EXTRACTION — UDYAM CERTIFICATE
  Fields extracted:
    udyam_number          : 19-character registration number
    enterprise_name       : Registered business/enterprise name
    owner_name            : Proprietor / partner name
    nic_code              : National Industrial Classification code
    nic_activity          : Business activity description
    category              : Micro / Small / Medium
    major_activity        : Manufacturing / Services / Trading
    registration_date     : Date of Udyam registration
    state_code            : 2-letter state code embedded in number
    date_of_commencement  : Business commencement date
    enterprise_type       : Proprietorship / Partnership / OPC / LLP


INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — UDYAM
  Field                 | Rule                                          | Blocking
  ----------------------|-----------------------------------------------|---------
  Udyam Number          | Must match UDYAM-XX-00-0000000 format        | HARD FAIL
                        | (regex: UDYAM-[A-Z]{2}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{7})    |
  State Code in Number  | 2-letter state code must be valid Indian state| HARD FAIL
                        | (e.g. TN = Tamil Nadu, MH = Maharashtra)      |
  API Status            | Must be Active                                | HARD FAIL
  Category              | Must be Micro, Small, or Medium               | HARD FAIL
  OCR Udyam Number      | Must match entered number exactly             | HARD FAIL
  OCR Enterprise Name   | Must match API enterprise_name (fuzzy ≥ 80%) | HARD FAIL
  Registration Date     | Must be a valid past date                     | Soft flag
  NIC Code              | Must be a valid 5-digit NIC code              | Soft flag
  Enterprise Type       | Must be valid legal entity type               | Soft flag


CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — UDYAM
  Check                                                    | Blocking
  ---------------------------------------------------------|---------
  Udyam number on certificate must match entered number    | HARD FAIL
  State code in Udyam number must match state in address   | Soft flag
  Enterprise name consistent across all certificate sections| Soft flag
  Registration date must not be in the future              | HARD FAIL
  Category (Micro/Small/Medium) consistent across sections | Soft flag


BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — UDYAM
  UDYAM registration has no direct payment/debit to check.
  However:
    Rule 1: If Step-5 has GST certificate with same GSTIN → cross-link
            gst_udyam_linked = true (strong P7 signal)
    Rule 2: Business income credits in Step-3 bank statement
            tagged as INCOME_GIG or INCOME_CASH may be cross-linked
            to Udyam activity type for income consistency scoring


GLOBAL VALIDATION — UDYAM
  Field               | Compared Against                       | Blocking
  --------------------|----------------------------------------|---------
  Enterprise Name     | Step-1 Full Name (fuzzy ≥ 80%)         | Soft flag
                      | (proprietor name may differ from biz   |
                      |  name — fuzzy match on owner_name used)|
  Owner Name          | Step-2 Aadhaar Name (fuzzy ≥ 85%)      | Soft flag
  State               | Step-1 State of Residence (exact)      | Soft flag
  Major Activity      | Step-1 Work Type alignment:            | Soft flag
                      |   Services → Freelancer / Tradesperson |
                      |   Trading → Vendor / Seller            |
  GSTIN Cross-link    | Step-5 GST cert GSTIN if available     | Soft flag
                      | If match → gst_udyam_linked = true     |

Example:
  Udyam owner_name   : Ravi Kumar
  Step-2 Aadhaar name: Ravi Kumar
  → fuzzy match ≥ 85% → PASS

  Udyam enterprise_name : Ravi Kumar Traders
  Step-1 Full Name      : Ravi Kumar
  → fuzzy match on owner_name used, not enterprise_name

Global profile update:
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.udyam = true
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.udyam_category = "Micro"
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.udyam_activity = "Services"
  verified_profile.gov_schemes.gst_udyam_linked = true


===========================================================================
GLOBAL VALIDATION — STEP-6 (AFTER ALL SELECTED SCHEMES)
===========================================================================

After all selected schemes pass individual and cross-internal validation,
the system updates the global profile object:

verified_profile.gov_schemes = {
  "eshram"           : true,
  "eshram_occupation": "Electrician",
  "pmsym"            : true,
  "pmsym_contribution": 100,
  "pmjjby"           : false,
  "pmmy"             : true,
  "mudra_loan_amount": 50000,
  "mudra_closed"     : false,
  "ppf"              : false,
  "udyam"            : true,
  "udyam_category"   : "Micro",
  "udyam_activity"   : "Services",
  "gst_udyam_linked" : true
}

Scheme participation count → scheme_count_norm feature (P7)
Each verified scheme adds to social_accountability_score.


===========================================================================
ML FEATURES PRODUCED — P7 SOCIAL ACCOUNTABILITY
===========================================================================

Feature                    | Description                              | Pillar
---------------------------|------------------------------------------|---------
eshram_active              | eShram registration verified (0/1)       | P7
pmsym_active               | PM-SYM pension active (0/1)              | P7, P2
pmjjby_active              | PMJJBY life insurance active (0/1)       | P7, P6
pmmy_completed             | Mudra loan verified (0/1)                | P7, P3
ppf_active                 | PPF contribution verified (0/1)          | P7, P4
udyam_registered           | Udyam/MSME registered (0/1)              | P7, P1
gst_udyam_linked           | GST cert linked to Udyam (0/1)           | P7, P1
scheme_count_norm          | Total verified schemes ÷ 6               | P7
scheme_bank_verified_ratio | Schemes with bank match ÷ total schemes  | P7, P2

Total features produced: 9 | Pillar: P7 (primary), P1, P2, P3, P4, P6


===========================================================================
TECHNICAL FLOW — STEP-6
===========================================================================

User opens Step-6
  ↓
Score impact message displayed
  ↓
User sees 6 scheme cards (all collapsed by default)
  ↓
[FOR EACH SCHEME USER SELECTS]

  User taps scheme card → expands
  User enters scheme ID (UAN / pension account / Udyam number etc.)
  User clicks Verify
    → API call made (if API available for this scheme)
    → API response stored temporarily
    → Document upload enabled on success
  User uploads proof document
    → OCR extraction runs on-device
    → Individual validation: OCR fields vs API response
    → Individual validation: format rules on all fields
  Cross-internal validation: consistency within document
  Bank cross-check: scheme payment matched in Step-3 CSV
  Global validation: identity fields vs Step-1 / Step-2 / Step-3
  Scheme marked: verified = true / unverified = false
  ↓
[AFTER ALL SELECTED SCHEMES PROCESSED]

  Global profile updated with all scheme flags
  ML features computed: scheme_count_norm, scheme_bank_verified_ratio
  ↓
Step-6 Status = COMPLETED
  ↓
User proceeds to Step-7 — Insurance Verification

--- specification folders_new\input vaidation and verification\step wise input validation\STEP-7-INSURANCE-VERIFICATION.txt ---
SYSTEM DESIGN PROMPT — STEP-7: INSURANCE VERIFICATION
GigCredit Onboarding | Version: Final | Pillar: P6 Financial Resilience
===========================================================================

PURPOSE
-------
Step-7 verifies the user's insurance coverage. Insurance participation
strengthens financial responsibility signals and improves risk assessment.
Three insurance types are supported:
  1. Health Insurance
  2. Vehicle Insurance
  3. Life Insurance

All insurance inputs are optional, EXCEPT Vehicle Insurance, which becomes
REQUIRED if the user selected "Yes" for Vehicle Ownership in Step-1.

Validation architecture:
  - OCR Extraction       : On-device
  - API Verification     : Government simulation (FastAPI + MongoDB)
  - Individual Validation: OCR vs API data (per field)
  - Cross-Internal       : Within document fields (consistency)
  - Global Validation    : Consistency with previous steps


===========================================================================
HOW IT IS PRESENTED ON SCREEN
===========================================================================

Three insurance cards displayed vertically.
Each card shows:
  - Insurance type name
  - Brief description of what it covers
  - Estimated score impact

Vehicle insurance card mandatory status is dynamically set based on
Vehicle Ownership answer from Step-1.


===========================================================================
INPUTS — STEP-7 (TOTAL: 3)
===========================================================================

#  | Field                                  | UI Element            | Format           | Count | Mandatory
---|----------------------------------------|-----------------------|------------------|-------|---------------------
1  | Health Insurance Policy Document       | Image/PDF upload card | JPG, PNG, PDF    | 1     | Optional
2  | Vehicle Insurance Policy               | Image/PDF upload card | JPG, PNG, PDF    | 1     | If Vehicle = Yes
3  | Life Insurance Policy Bond or          | Image/PDF upload card | JPG, PNG, PDF    | 1     | Optional
   | Premium Receipt                        |                       |                  |       |

Total Inputs  : 3
Mandatory     : 0–1 (vehicle insurance conditional on Step-1 answer)
Optional      : 2–3
Document uploads: Up to 3 images/PDFs


===========================================================================
UI GATE RULES
===========================================================================

GATE 1 — Vehicle Insurance Card Badge (dynamic from Step-1):
  verified_profile.has_vehicle = true
    → Card shows "REQUIRED" badge
    → Upload is mandatory
    → Missing = incomplete step

  verified_profile.has_vehicle = false
    → Card shows "OPTIONAL" badge
    → Upload not required
    → Skipping has no penalty

GATE 2 — Continue Button:
  has_vehicle = true  → vehicle insurance must be uploaded and pass
                        all validations before Continue is enabled
  has_vehicle = false → Continue available at any point (all 3 optional)

GATE 3 — RC Deduplication (Step-5 cross-dependency):
  IF verified_profile.work.rc_verified = true (Step-5A completed):
    → RC verification SKIPPED in Step-7
    → System reuses vehicle_number from Step-5 global state
    → Vehicle insurance OCR vehicle number cross-checked against
      Step-5 vehicle number
  IF step5_skipped = true OR rc_verified = false:
    → Normal RC verification flow applies in Step-7


===========================================================================
INSURANCE 1 — HEALTH INSURANCE
===========================================================================

Score impact shown on card: "Can add up to 18 points"

--- OCR EXTRACTION — HEALTH INSURANCE POLICY ---

Field                | Example Value              | Used For
---------------------|----------------------------|-------------------------------
policy_number        | P700002012015007530        | API verification
policy_holder_name   | Ashwani Kumar Rai          | API + Step-1 + Step-2 match
insurance_company    | Star Health                | API verification
policy_start_date    | 2014-07-03                 | Active period check
policy_expiry_date   | 2015-07-02                 | Active status check
sum_insured          | 300000                     | P6 feature: health_sum_norm
premium_amount       | 6067                       | Bank transaction cross-check
scheme_description   | 2 Adults + 1 Child         | Coverage type signal

INSURER DETECTION:
  System detects insurer name from document.
  Example: "Star Health and Allied Insurance"

--- API VERIFICATION — HEALTH INSURANCE ---

Endpoint  : POST /gov/insurance/health-policy-verify
MongoDB   : health_insurance_db
Trigger   : Automatic after OCR extracts policy_number

Request:
  {
    "policy_number"      : "P700002012015007530",
    "insurance_company"  : "Star Health",
    "policy_holder_name" : "Ashwani Kumar Rai"
  }

Response:
  {
    "policy_number"      : "P700002012015007530",
    "policy_holder_name" : "Ashwani Kumar Rai",
    "insurance_company"  : "Star Health",
    "policy_status"      : "Expired",
    "policy_expiry_date" : "2015-07-02",
    "sum_insured"        : 300000,
    "verified"           : true
  }

Possible policy_status values: Active | Expired | Not Found

--- INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — HEALTH INSURANCE ---

#  | Field                    | Rule                            | Blocking
---|--------------------------|----------------------------------|----------
1  | Policy Number            | OCR must match API               | HARD FAIL
2  | Policy Holder Name       | OCR vs API fuzzy >= 85%          | HARD FAIL
3  | Insurance Company        | OCR must match API               | HARD FAIL
4  | Policy Start Date        | Must be valid date format        | HARD FAIL
5  | Policy Expiry Date       | Valid date; API status = Active  | HARD FAIL
6  | Sum Insured              | Must be numeric > 0              | HARD FAIL
7  | Premium Amount           | Must be numeric > 0              | HARD FAIL
8  | Scheme Description       | Must be readable (not blank)     | Soft flag

--- CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — HEALTH INSURANCE ---

#  | Check                                          | Rule               | Blocking
---|------------------------------------------------|---------------------|---------
1  | Policy number consistent across document       | Identical in all   | HARD FAIL
   | sections                                       | sections           |
2  | Policy holder name consistent across document  | Same in all name   | HARD FAIL
   |                                                | fields             |

--- BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — HEALTH INSURANCE ---

Premium debit matching in Step-3 canonical CSV:
  Amount   : premium_amount (OCR) ± 5%
             OR annual_premium ÷ 12 (if monthly mode) ± 5%
  Merchant : contains insurer name keywords
             e.g. "STAR HEALTH", "HDFC ERGO", "NIVA BUPA",
                  "CARE HEALTH", "ICICI LOMBARD", "HEALTH INSURANCE"
  Category : INSURANCE_PREMIUM

  Match found → health_premium_bank_verified = true  (bonus P6 signal)
  No match    → health_premium_bank_verified = false  (soft flag, non-blocking)

  Note: Step-3 bank statement is converted to a canonical CSV format.
  Each transaction has: date, amount, merchant, category, transaction_id.
  Matching uses: transaction date ± 7 days of premium due date AND
                 amount within ± 5% tolerance.

--- GLOBAL VALIDATION — HEALTH INSURANCE ---

#  | Check                                          | Compared Against        | Blocking
---|------------------------------------------------|--------------------------|----------
1  | Policy holder name (OCR)                       | verified_profile.name    | HARD FAIL
   |                                                | (Step-1 full name)       |
2  | Policy holder name (OCR)                       | verified_profile.        | HARD FAIL
   |                                                | identity.aadhaar_name    |
   |                                                | (Step-2 Aadhaar name)    |

Score impact shown on card: "Can add up to 12 points"

Dynamic Behaviour:
  Vehicle Ownership in Step-1 = Yes → Card shows REQUIRED badge

  Vehicle Ownership in Step-1 = No  → Card shows OPTIONAL badge
                                       Upload not required, no penalty

Vehicle insurance is verified in TWO stages:
  Stage 1: RC Book Verification
  Stage 2: Vehicle Insurance Policy Verification

--- STAGE 1: RC BOOK VERIFICATION ---

RC Deduplication Check (run first):
  IF verified_profile.work.rc_verified = true (from Step-5A):
    → SKIP RC API call
    → Reuse from global state:
        vehicle_number = verified_profile.work.vehicle_number
        owner_name     = verified_profile.work.rc_owner_name
        vehicle_class  = verified_profile.work.vehicle_class
    → Show user: "RC already verified in Step-5. Proceeding to
                  insurance check."
  ELSE:
    → User uploads RC Book document
    → Run OCR + API verification (below)

===========================================================================
TECHNICAL FLOW — STEP-7
===========================================================================

User opens Step-7
  ↓
System reads verified_profile.has_vehicle from Step-1
  ↓
Three insurance cards rendered with correct badges (REQUIRED / OPTIONAL)
  ↓
[HEALTH INSURANCE — Optional]
  User uploads Health Insurance Policy Document
    → OCR: policy_number, holder_name, company, start_date,
            expiry_date, sum_insured, premium_amount, scheme_desc
    → POST /gov/insurance/health-policy-verify
    → Individual validation: all 8 OCR fields validated (see table)
    → Cross-internal: policy_number + holder_name consistent
    → Bank cross-check: premium debit in Step-3 CSV (± 5%, ± 7 days)
    → Global: holder_name vs Step-1 name AND Step-2 Aadhaar name
    [PASS] → health_insurance = true
    [FAIL] → card shows error; user can retry; step not blocked
  ↓
[VEHICLE INSURANCE — Mandatory if has_vehicle = true]

  GATE: IF rc_verified = true from Step-5A:
    → Skip RC API, reuse vehicle_number from global state
  ELSE:
    → User uploads RC Book
    → OCR: vehicle_number, owner_name, vehicle_class,
            registration_date, engine_number, chassis_number
    → POST /gov/vehicle/rc-verify
    → RC Individual validation: all 6 fields (see table)
  ↓
  User uploads Vehicle Insurance Policy
    → OCR: policy_number, vehicle_number, owner_name, company,
            start_date, expiry_date, premium_amount,
            chassis_number, engine_number
    → POST /gov/insurance/vehicle-policy-verify
    → Individual validation: all 9 fields (see table)
    → Cross-internal: vehicle_number, chassis_number, engine_number
                      consistent across RC + insurance
    → Bank cross-check: premium debit in Step-3 CSV
    → Global: vehicle_number vs Step-5 vehicle_number (if applicable)
    [PASS] → vehicle_insurance = true
    [FAIL] → HARD FAIL if has_vehicle = true (blocks Continue)
  ↓
[LIFE INSURANCE — Optional]
  User uploads Life Insurance Policy or Premium Receipt
    → OCR: policy_number, holder_name, company, sum_assured,
            total_premium_paid, issue_date, coverage_period,
            coverage_status, payment_mode, coverage_name
    → POST /gov/insurance/life-policy-verify
    → Individual validation: all 9 fields (see table)
    → Cross-internal: policy_number + holder_name consistent
    → Bank cross-check: premium debit in Step-3 CSV (± 5%, ± 7 days)
    → Global: holder_name vs Step-1 name AND Step-2 Aadhaar name
    [PASS] → life_insurance = true
    [FAIL] → card shows error; user can retry; step not blocked
  ↓
Update verified_profile.insurance
  ↓
Step-7 Status = COMPLETED
Enable navigation to Step-8

--- specification folders_new\input vaidation and verification\step wise input validation\STEP-8-ITR-AND-GST-RECORDS.txt ---
SYSTEM DESIGN PROMPT — STEP-8: ITR AND GST RECORDS
GigCredit Onboarding | Version: Final | Pillars: P1, P6, P7, Meta-Learner
===========================================================================

PURPOSE
-------
Step-8 collects formal income declaration and tax compliance records.
ITR and GST documents independently verify the income reported in the
bank statement (Step-3) and the income declared in Step-1.

These are the strongest formal income signals in the system. Submitting
them improves confidence scores across P1 (Income Stability), P4
(Savings Behaviour), P6 (Financial Resilience), and the Meta-Learner.

All inputs in this step are optional. The user may skip either section
or both. However, if the user provides any document, it must pass
all validation layers. Invalid inputs are rejected and not used.

Validation architecture:
  - OCR Extraction       : On-device
  - API Verification     : Government simulation (FastAPI + MongoDB)
  - Individual Validation: Per-field rules on OCR extracted data
  - Cross-Internal       : Consistency within and across documents
  - Global Validation    : Consistency with all previous verified steps


===========================================================================
HOW IT IS PRESENTED ON SCREEN
===========================================================================

Two separate sections on one screen:
  - Section A: ITR (Income Tax Return) — shown on top
  - Section B: GST (Goods and Services Tax) — shown below

Both sections are collapsed by default.

Score impact message shown at top of screen:
  "ITR and GST records are the strongest proof of income.
   Submitting them can improve your score by up to 22 points
   across multiple pillars."

GST section is VISUALLY EXPANDED and PROMINENTLY DISPLAYED
by default for:
  - Vendor Seller (Step-1 work type)
  - Freelancer (Step-1 work type)

GST section remains collapsed for:
  - Platform Worker
  - Skilled Tradesperson


===========================================================================
INPUTS — STEP-8 (TOTAL: 4, ALL OPTIONAL)
===========================================================================

#  | Field                              | UI Element            | Format         | Count | Mandatory
---|------------------------------------|-----------------------|----------------|-------|----------
1  | ITR Acknowledgement Receipt        | Image/PDF upload card | JPG, PNG, PDF  | 1     | Optional
   | (Form V)                           |                       |                |       |
2  | Form 26AS                          | PDF upload card       | PDF only       | 1     | Optional
3  | GSTIN Number                       | Text field (uppercase)| 15-char        | 1     | Optional
   |                                    |                       | alphanumeric   |       |
4  | GSTR-3B Monthly Returns            | Multi-file PDF upload | Up to 6 PDFs   | 1–6   | Optional

Total Inputs  : 4
Mandatory     : 0
Optional      : 4
Document uploads: Up to 8 (1 Form V + 1 Form 26AS + 6 GSTR-3B returns)

UI Gate Rule:
  → If user uploads Form V but NOT Form 26AS: acceptable, Form 26AS optional.
  → If user uploads Form 26AS but NOT Form V: acceptable, Form V optional.
  → If user enters GSTIN but uploads NO GSTR-3B: reject GSTIN alone;
    at least 1 GSTR-3B return required to use GSTIN for scoring.
  → If GSTIN not entered: GSTR-3B upload blocked (greyed out).


===========================================================================
SECTION A — ITR (INCOME TAX RETURN) VERIFICATION
===========================================================================

Score impact: Up to 15 points across P1 and P6.

The user may upload one or both of the following documents:
  1. ITR Acknowledgement Receipt (Form V / ITR-V)
  2. Form 26AS (Annual Statement)

Both documents are independent. Either alone is accepted.
Both together produce more features.

--- OCR EXTRACTION — ITR FORM V (ACKNOWLEDGEMENT RECEIPT) ---

Field                  | Example Value          | Used For
-----------------------|------------------------|-------------------------------
taxpayer_name          | Ravi Kumar             | Step-1 + Step-2 name match
pan_number             | ABCDE1234F             | Step-2 PAN cross-check
assessment_year        | 2024-25                | Filing recency check
acknowledgement_number | 123456789012345        | API verification (15 digits)
date_of_filing         | 2025-07-18             | Filing window validation
total_income           | 480000                 | P1 + P6 feature, bank match
gross_total_income     | 510000                 | Income before deductions
deductions             | 30000                  | Deduction signal
net_taxable_income     | 480000                 | Final taxable income

--- OCR EXTRACTION — FORM 26AS ---

Field                            | Used For
---------------------------------|-------------------------------
taxpayer_name                    | Name consistency with Form V
pan_number                       | PAN consistency with Form V
total_tds_amount                 | Tax deduction signal (P6)
total_income_from_all_sources    | Cross-check with Form V income
employer_or_client_tan_details   | Formal income source signal
advance_tax_payments             | Financial discipline signal

--- API VERIFICATION — ITR ---

Endpoint  : POST /gov/income-tax/itr-verify
MongoDB   : itr_filings_db
Trigger   : Automatic after OCR extracts acknowledgement_number

Request:
  {
    "pan_number"             : "ABCDE1234F",
    "acknowledgement_number" : "123456789012345",
    "assessment_year"        : "2024-25"
  }

Response:
  {
    "itr_found"      : true,
    "filing_status"  : "Valid",
    "assessed_income": 480000,
    "verified"       : true
  }

Possible filing_status values: Valid | Invalid Filing | Not Found

AIS BACKEND CHECK (SIMULATED):
  Backend performs simulated cross-check with Annual Information
  Statement (AIS) data for high-value transaction anomaly detection.
  If declared ITR income deviates > 50% from AIS estimated income:
    → Flag: income_ais_mismatch = true (non-blocking, soft signal only)

--- INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — ITR FORM V ---

#  | Field                    | Rule                                         | Blocking
---|--------------------------|----------------------------------------------|---------
1  | Taxpayer Name            | Min 2 words, alphabets + spaces only,        | HARD FAIL
   |                          | no numbers, min 3 characters                 |
2  | PAN Number               | Regex: [A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z]{1},            | HARD FAIL
   |                          | exactly 10 characters, all uppercase         |
3  | Assessment Year          | Valid FY format (e.g. 2024-25);              | HARD FAIL
   |                          | not a future AY; within last 3 AYs           |
4  | Acknowledgement Number   | Exactly 15 digits, numeric only              | HARD FAIL
5  | Total Income             | Must be numeric, > 0                         | HARD FAIL
6  | Date of Filing           | Must be valid date; must fall within          | HARD FAIL
   |                          | valid ITR filing window for that AY           |
   |                          | (typically April–December of filing year)    |

--- INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — FORM 26AS ---

#  | Field                           | Rule                       | Blocking
---|---------------------------------|----------------------------|---------
1  | Taxpayer Name                   | Must be readable, alphabets| HARD FAIL
2  | PAN Number                      | Same regex as Form V       | HARD FAIL
3  | Total TDS Amount                | Numeric ≥ 0                | HARD FAIL
4  | Total Income from All Sources   | Numeric ≥ 0                | HARD FAIL

--- CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — ITR ---

#  | Check                                              | Rule                     | Blocking
---|----------------------------------------------------|--------------------------|---------
1  | Taxpayer name consistent within Form V             | Fuzzy ≥ 85%              | HARD FAIL
2  | Assessment Year ↔ Filing Date                      | Filing date must fall    | HARD FAIL
   |                                                    | within valid period for  |
   |                                                    | that AY                  |
3  | IF both Form V AND Form 26AS uploaded:             |                          |
   |   PAN Number (Form V vs Form 26AS)                 | Must be identical        | HARD FAIL
   |   Taxpayer Name (Form V vs Form 26AS)              | Fuzzy ≥ 85%              | HARD FAIL
   |   Total Income (Form V vs Form 26AS)               | Within ± 30% tolerance   | Soft flag

--- BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — ITR ---

Income consistency validation using Step-3 canonical CSV:
  Formula:
    expected_monthly = total_income (ITR) ÷ 12
    lower_bound      = expected_monthly × 0.60
    upper_bound      = expected_monthly × 1.40

  Step-3 average monthly credit = avg monthly bank deposits

  IF avg_monthly_credit is within [lower_bound, upper_bound]:
    → income_consistency = "strong"
    → itr_to_bank_ratio feature = computed
  IF avg_monthly_credit is outside range:
    → income_consistency = "medium" (non-blocking, does NOT reject ITR)
    → Soft flag only; ITR still accepted

  Note: Bank statement is converted to canonical CSV in Step-3.
  Each transaction has: date, amount, merchant, category, transaction_id.

--- GLOBAL VALIDATION — ITR ---

#  | Check                          | Compared Against                    | Blocking
---|--------------------------------|-------------------------------------|---------
1  | Taxpayer Name (OCR)            | verified_profile.name (Step-1)      | HARD FAIL
2  | Taxpayer Name (OCR)            | verified_profile.identity.pan_name  | HARD FAIL
   |                                | (Step-2 PAN API name)               |
3  | PAN Number (OCR)               | verified_profile.identity.          | HARD FAIL
   |                                | pan_number (Step-2)                 |
4  | ITR declared income ÷ 12       | Step-3 avg monthly bank credits     | Soft flag
   |                                | (60%–140% tolerance band)           |


===========================================================================
SECTION B — GST RECORDS VERIFICATION
===========================================================================

Score impact: Up to 7 points in P7 (Social Accountability) and P1.

The user:
  1. Enters GSTIN Number (15-character)
  2. Uploads up to 6 GSTR-3B Monthly Return PDFs

GSTIN entry unlocks the GSTR-3B multi-file upload slot.
If GSTIN is not entered, GSTR-3B upload remains disabled (greyed out).
Minimum 1 GSTR-3B return required to register any GST score impact.

--- OCR EXTRACTION — GSTR-3B (PER RETURN, UP TO 6 FILES) ---

Field                             | Example Value  | Used For
----------------------------------|----------------|-------------------------------
gstin                             | 33AABCU9603R1Z5| API verification
business_legal_name               | Ravi Kumar     | Step-1 name cross-check
return_filing_month               | Jan-2026       | Filing consistency check
outward_taxable_supplies_turnover | 65000          | P1 income feature
filing_date                       | 2026-02-12     | Recency and regularity check
tax_payable                       | 11700          | Tax compliance signal
tax_paid                          | 11700          | Payment discipline signal

--- API VERIFICATION — GST ---

Endpoint  : POST /gov/gst/gstin-verify
MongoDB   : gstin_db
Trigger   : After user enters GSTIN and taps Verify

Request:
  { "gstin": "33AABCU9603R1Z5" }

Response:
  {
    "gstin_found"         : true,
    "business_legal_name" : "Ravi Kumar",
    "gstin_status"        : "Active",
    "registration_date"   : "2022-04-01",
    "verified"            : true
  }

Possible gstin_status values: Active | Cancelled | Suspended | Not Found

GSTR-3B FILING CONSISTENCY API CHECK (after OCR):
  Endpoint  : POST /gov/gst/filing-history
  MongoDB   : gstr3b_filings_db
  Request   : { "gstin": "33AABCU9603R1Z5" }
  Response  :
    {
      "months_filed"     : 5,
      "latest_filing"    : "2026-01-12",
      "consistency_flag" : "regular"
    }

--- INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — GST ---

#  | Field                            | Rule                              | Blocking
---|----------------------------------|-----------------------------------|---------
1  | GSTIN                            | Exactly 15 alphanumeric chars;    | HARD FAIL
   |                                  | first 2 digits = state code;      |
   |                                  | matches PAN in chars 3–12;        |
   |                                  | API status = Active               |
2  | Business Legal Name              | Must be readable; alphabets +     | HARD FAIL
   |                                  | spaces; min 2 chars               |
3  | Return Filing Month              | Must be a valid month-year format | HARD FAIL
   |                                  | (e.g. Jan-2026, 01-2026)         |
4  | Monthly Turnover                 | Must be numeric, ≥ 0              | HARD FAIL
5  | Filing Date                      | Must be valid date                | HARD FAIL
6  | Tax Payable                      | Must be numeric, ≥ 0              | HARD FAIL
7  | Tax Paid                         | Must be numeric, ≥ 0;            | HARD FAIL
   |                                  | ≤ tax_payable + 5% tolerance     |

--- CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — GST ---

#  | Check                                              | Rule               | Blocking
---|----------------------------------------------------|--------------------|----------
1  | GSTIN consistent across all uploaded GSTR-3B files | Must be identical  | HARD FAIL
2  | Business Legal Name consistent across all files    | Fuzzy ≥ 85%        | HARD FAIL
3  | Filing months must represent distinct months       | No duplicate months| HARD FAIL
4  | Filing months must be in chronological order       | Ascending dates    | Soft flag
5  | Gap between filing months must be ≤ 35 days        | Consecutive months | Soft flag
   | (regularity check)                                 |                    |

--- BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — GST ---

GST tax payment verification in Step-3 canonical CSV:
  Merchant  : contains GST-related keywords
              e.g. "GST", "GSTIN", "TAX PAYMENT", "IGST", "CGST", "SGST"
  Amount    : tax_paid (OCR) ± 5% tolerance
  Date      : ± 10 days of filing date

  Match found → gst_tax_bank_verified = true  (bonus P7 signal)
  No match    → gst_tax_bank_verified = false  (soft flag, non-blocking)

--- GLOBAL VALIDATION — GST ---

#  | Check                          | Compared Against                    | Blocking
---|--------------------------------|-------------------------------------|---------
1  | Business Legal Name (OCR)      | verified_profile.name (Step-1)      | Soft flag
   |                                | (Mismatch allowed if trading name   |
   |                                |  differs from personal name;        |
   |                                |  flagged but not rejected)          |
2  | GSTIN (Step-8)                 | Step-5 GST certificate GSTIN        | HARD FAIL
   |                                | (if Vendor/Freelancer submitted     |
   |                                |  GST cert in Step-5)                |
3  | Monthly turnover               | Step-3 avg monthly bank credits     | Soft flag
   |                                | (± 30% tolerance)                   |

----------------------|----------------------------------------------------

                      | tip shown: "GST registration shows formal
                      | business operation."
Freelancer            | Expanded by default; tip shown: "GST registration
                      | confirms professional income."
Platform Worker       | Collapsed; expandable; low emphasis
Skilled Tradesperson  | Collapsed; expandable; low emphasis

After all validations pass, the global profile is updated:

verified_profile.itr = {
  "pan"              : "ABCDE1234F",
  "assessment_year"  : "2024-25",
  "declared_income"  : 480000,
  "itr_verified"     : true,
  "form_26as_linked" : true
}

verified_profile.gst = {
  "gstin"              : "33AABCU9603R1Z5",
  "gst_registered"     : true,
  "filing_consistency" : 5,    // months out of 6 uploaded
  "avg_monthly_turnover": 62000
}


===========================================================================
ML FEATURES PRODUCED — STEP-8
===========================================================================

Feature                     | Description                            | Pillar
----------------------------|----------------------------------------|-------
itr_filed_binary            | ITR verified and filed (0/1)           | P6, Meta
declared_income_ratio       | ITR annual ÷ 12 ÷ bank monthly avg     | P1
itr_to_bank_ratio           | Income consistency cross-check ratio   | P1, P6

gst_monthly_turnover_norm   | Avg monthly turnover ÷ income anchor   | P1 (Vendor/
                            |                                        | Freelancer)

Total features produced: 6
Pillars impacted: P1, P6, P7, Meta-Learner


===========================================================================
TECHNICAL FLOW — STEP-8
===========================================================================

User opens Step-8
  ↓
Step-level score message displayed
  ↓
System reads Step-1 work type:
  Vendor / Freelancer → GST section expanded by default
  Platform Worker / Tradesperson → GST section collapsed
  ↓
[SECTION A — ITR]

  [IF user uploads ITR Form V]
    → OCR: taxpayer_name, pan_number, assessment_year,
            acknowledgement_number, date_of_filing,
            total_income, gross_total_income,
            deductions, net_taxable_income
    → POST /gov/income-tax/itr-verify
    → Individual validation: all 6 Form V fields (see table)
    → AIS backend simulated cross-check

  [IF user uploads Form 26AS]
    → OCR: taxpayer_name, pan_number, total_tds_amount,
            total_income_from_all_sources,
            employer_tan_details, advance_tax_payments
    → Individual validation: all 4 Form 26AS fields (see table)

  [IF BOTH uploaded]
    → Cross-internal: PAN match, name fuzzy match, income ± 30%

  → Bank cross-check: ITR income vs Step-3 avg monthly credits
    (60%–140% tolerance band)
  → Global: taxpayer_name vs Step-1 name, Step-2 PAN name, Step-2 PAN number
  [PASS] → itr_verified = true
  [FAIL] → card shows error; user can retry; step not blocked overall
  ↓
[SECTION B — GST]

  [IF user enters GSTIN]
    → POST /gov/gst/gstin-verify
    → GSTIN individual validation (format + API status = Active)
    → GSTR-3B upload slot enabled
  ↓
  [IF user uploads GSTR-3B returns (1–6 files)]
    → OCR per file: gstin, business_legal_name, return_filing_month,
                    outward_taxable_supplies, filing_date,
                    tax_payable, tax_paid
    → POST /gov/gst/filing-history (filing consistency check)
    → Individual validation: all 7 GSTR-3B fields per file (see table)
    → Cross-internal: GSTIN consistent, business name consistent,
                      distinct months, no duplicates
    → Bank cross-check: GST tax payment in Step-3 CSV
    → Global: business name vs Step-1 name (soft);
              GSTIN vs Step-5 GST cert GSTIN (hard, if applicable)
    [PASS] → gst_registered = true; filing_consistency computed
    [FAIL] → card shows error; user can retry; step not blocked overall
  ↓
Update verified_profile.itr AND verified_profile.gst
  ↓
Step-8 Status = COMPLETED
  ↓
Proceed to Final Credit Scoring and Report Generation

OUTPUT OF STEP-8
===========================================================================

Step-8 Status           : COMPLETED
verified_profile update : itr and gst objects updated
P1 + P6 + P7 features  : 6 ML features produced
Next Step               : Final Credit Score Computation and Report
