--- specification folders_new\input vaidation and verification\step wise input validation\STEP-9-EMI-AND-LOAN-OBLIGATIONS.txt ---
SYSTEM DESIGN PROMPT — STEP-9: EMI AND LOAN OBLIGATIONS
GigCredit Onboarding | Version: Final | Pillar: P3 Debt Management
===========================================================================

PURPOSE
-------
Step-9 captures the user's existing debt obligations and repayment
behaviour. This is the FINAL onboarding step. EMI and loan data feeds
the P3 (Debt Management) scoring pillar and cross-validates against
the bank statement dataset collected in Step-3.

The primary verification method is bank transaction cross-check.
An optional API trigger provides additional verification signal.
If the user has no active loans, they may declare "No active loans"
and proceed directly.

Validation architecture:
  - Individual Validation : Per-field rules on user-entered data
  - API Verification      : Optional loan verification (FastAPI + MongoDB)
  - Bank Cross-Check      : EMI matching against Step-3 canonical CSV
  - Cross-Internal        : Consistency across multiple loan entries
  - Global Validation     : Declared loans vs auto-detected EMIs in Step-3


===========================================================================
HOW IT IS PRESENTED ON SCREEN
===========================================================================

A single screen with:
  - A "No Active Loans" toggle at the top
  - A primary loan entry card (shown by default)
  - An "Add Another Loan" button below (adds up to 5 additional loans)
  - A Verify button per loan card (optional API trigger)

Score impact message shown at top of screen:
  "Declaring your active loans helps us accurately assess your
   debt burden. Honest disclosure improves your score reliability."

No Active Loans Toggle:
  User selects "I have no active loans"
    → All loan fields hidden
    → Step-9 marked as declared_no_loans = true
    → System still runs auto-detection from Step-3 bank data
    → If EMI-tagged debits found in Step-3 → undisclosed_emi_flag = true
    → Step-9 COMPLETED (no blocking)

Maximum loans allowed: 6 (1 primary + 5 additional)


===========================================================================
INPUTS — STEP-9 (5 FIELDS PER LOAN ENTRY)
===========================================================================

Per Loan Entry:

#  | Field                     | UI Element              | Format                    | Mandatory
---|---------------------------|-------------------------|---------------------------|----------
1  | Lender Name               | Text field / Dropdown   | Free text or predefined   | Required
   |                           |                         | list of Indian lenders    |
2  | Monthly EMI Amount (INR)  | Numeric text field      | Positive integer, no      | Required
   |                           | with ₹ prefix           | decimals                  |
3  | Previous Debit Date       | Date picker             | DD-MM-YYYY                | Required
4  | Latest Debit Date         | Date picker             | DD-MM-YYYY                | Required
5  | Loan Verification API     | "Verify" button         | Tap to trigger            | Optional
   | Trigger                   | per loan card           | API call                  | (action)

Total Inputs per loan entry : 5 (4 data fields + 1 optional action)
Total loan entries allowed  : Up to 6
Total mandatory inputs      : 4 per loan × number of loans declared
Total optional              : Verify button (per loan)

Predefined Lender Dropdown options (examples):
  HDFC Bank, ICICI Bank, SBI, Axis Bank, Bajaj Finserv, Muthoot Finance,
  Shriram Finance, IIFL Finance, Fullerton India, KreditBee, MoneyTap,
  PaySense, EarlySalary, Navi, Kreditech, Other (free text entry)

UI Note:
  Previous Debit Date and Latest Debit Date pickers show:
  "Enter the last two months EMI debit dates from your bank statement."


===========================================================================
INDIVIDUAL VALIDATION — PER LOAN ENTRY
===========================================================================

#  | Field                  | Rule                                          | Blocking
---|------------------------|-----------------------------------------------|---------
1  | Lender Name            | Min 3 characters                              | HARD FAIL
   |                        | Alphabets + spaces only                       |
   |                        | Must not be blank                             |
2  | Monthly EMI Amount     | Must be numeric                               | HARD FAIL
   |                        | Must be > 0                                   |
   |                        | Must be ≤ Step-1 declared monthly income      |
   |                        | (EMI cannot exceed total income)              |
3  | Previous Debit Date    | Must be valid date format (DD-MM-YYYY)        | HARD FAIL
   |                        | Must be earlier than Latest Debit Date        |
   |                        | Must not be a future date                     |
4  | Latest Debit Date      | Must be valid date format (DD-MM-YYYY)        | HARD FAIL
   |                        | Must not be a future date                     |
   |                        | Must be within the last 90 days               |
5  | EMI Frequency Sanity   | Gap between Previous and Latest Debit Date    | Soft flag
   |                        | must be between 25 and 35 days                |
   |                        | (monthly cycle check)                         |
   |                        | If gap < 25 or > 35: flag as irregular cycle  |


===========================================================================
API VERIFICATION — LOAN (OPTIONAL TRIGGER)
===========================================================================

User taps "Verify" button on a loan card to trigger verification.
This is optional. If not triggered, bank cross-check is used instead.

Endpoint  : POST /gov/loan/verify
MongoDB   : loan_obligations_db
Trigger   : User taps Verify button per loan card

Request:
  {
    "lender_name"      : "HDFC Bank",
    "emi_amount"       : 5000,
    "latest_debit_date": "2026-04-05"
  }

Response:
  {
    "loan_found"          : true,
    "loan_status"         : "Active",
    "outstanding_balance" : 85000,
    "loan_type"           : "Personal Loan",
    "verified"            : true
  }

Possible loan_status values: Active | Closed | Defaulted | Not Found

Post-API Individual Validation:
  IF loan_status = Active  → loan_api_verified = true
  IF loan_status = Closed  → flag: loan_declared_as_closed = true
                             (user declared active, API says closed)
                             Soft flag, non-blocking
  IF loan_status = Defaulted → flag: loan_defaulted = true
                               Hard negative P3 signal; step NOT blocked
  IF loan_status = Not Found → API verification inconclusive;
                               fallback to bank cross-check only

Outstanding balance stored as:
  verified_profile.loans[i].outstanding_balance
  Used to compute: debt_to_income_ratio (P3 feature)


===========================================================================
BANK TRANSACTION CROSS-CHECK — PRIMARY VERIFICATION METHOD
===========================================================================

This is the MAIN verification method for Step-9.
Each declared EMI is matched against the Step-3 canonical CSV.

Step-3 canonical CSV structure per transaction:
  date | amount | merchant_narration | category | transaction_id

Matching rules per loan entry:

LATEST DEBIT MATCH:
  Amount   : transaction amount = EMI amount ± 5% tolerance
  Date     : transaction date = Latest Debit Date ± 5 days
  Merchant : transaction narration contains lender keywords
             e.g. "HDFC", "BAJAJ", "EMI", "LOAN", "EQUATED",
                  "INSTALMENT", "ECS", "NACH", "AUTO DEBIT"
  Category : tagged as EMI in Step-3 transaction parser

PREVIOUS DEBIT MATCH:
  Amount   : same EMI amount ± 5%
  Date     : ~30 days before Latest Debit Date ± 5 days

MATCH RESULTS:

  Both Latest AND Previous debit found:
    → emi_bank_verified = true
    → emi_recurring     = true  (strongest P3 positive signal)

  Only Latest debit found:
    → emi_bank_verified = true
    → emi_recurring     = false  (single confirmation)

  No debit found:
    → emi_bank_verified = false
    → EMI still accepted as self-declared
    → Soft flag: emi_unverified = true
    → Negative P3 signal but NON-BLOCKING

  EMI debit found in Step-3 but amount differs by > 30%:
    → Soft flag: emi_amount_mismatch = true
    → Step NOT blocked; discrepancy recorded as P3 signal


===========================================================================
UNDISCLOSED EMI AUTO-DETECTION (GLOBAL STEP-3 SCAN)
===========================================================================

The system scans ALL Step-3 bank transactions tagged as category EMI.
It compares auto-detected EMI patterns against user-declared loans.

Detection logic:
  1. Collect all transactions in Step-3 where category = EMI
  2. Group recurring debits:
     same amount (± 5%) + same merchant + recurring monthly pattern
     → identify as a distinct EMI obligation
  3. For each auto-detected EMI group:
     a. Check if user declared a loan with matching lender + amount
     b. If matched → emi_declared = true
     c. If NOT matched → undisclosed_emi detected

Undisclosed EMI Rule:
  IF auto-detected EMI NOT declared by user:
    → undisclosed_emi_flag = true
    → undisclosed_emi_count += 1
    → Negative P3 signal
    → Step-9 NOT blocked (user can still complete onboarding)
    → Soft flag only

  Example:
    Step-3 shows: recurring ₹3,200 debit to "BAJAJ FINSERV EMI"
                  on 7th of each month for 6 months.
    User did NOT declare this loan in Step-9.
    → System flags: undisclosed_emi_flag = true
    → Negative scoring signal applied silently in P3.


===========================================================================
CROSS-INTERNAL VALIDATION — ACROSS ALL LOAN ENTRIES
===========================================================================

#  | Check                               | Rule                          | Blocking
---|-------------------------------------|-------------------------------|---------
1  | Total EMI burden vs income          | Sum of all declared EMIs must | Soft flag
   |                                     | be < Step-1 declared monthly  |
   |                                     | income                        |
   |                                     | IF > 80% of income:           |
   |                                     | flag high_emi_burden = true   |
2  | Duplicate lender check              | Same lender name cannot appear| HARD FAIL
   |                                     | more than once across all     |
   |                                     | loan entries                  |
3  | Debit date consistency              | Previous Debit Date must be   | Soft flag
   |                                     | ~30 days (25–35 days) before  |
   |                                     | Latest Debit Date for EACH    |
   |                                     | loan entry                    |
4  | EMI-to-income ratio computation     | total_emi ÷ declared_income   | Feature
   |                                     | = emi_burden_ratio            | only
   |                                     | Stored as P3 feature          |
5  | EMI declared vs Step-3 auto-detect  | Declared count vs auto-       | Soft flag
   |                                     | detected EMI groups from      |
   |                                     | Step-3 bank data              |


===========================================================================
GLOBAL VALIDATION — STEP-9
===========================================================================

#  | Check                                   | Compared Against              | Blocking
---|-----------------------------------------|-------------------------------|---------
1  | Total EMI debits in bank                | Step-3 EMI-tagged transaction | Soft flag
   |                                         | total vs declared total EMI   |
2  | Auto-detected EMIs (Step-3) vs          | User-declared loans in Step-9 | Soft flag
   | declared EMIs (Step-9)                  | (undisclosed_emi_flag rule)   |
3  | Outstanding balance vs bank debits      | If API verified: outstanding  | Soft flag
   |                                         | balance consistency with      |
   |                                         | Step-3 debit history          |
4  | EMI lender vs known NACH/ECS merchants  | Cross-check lender name       | Soft flag
   |                                         | against NACH/ECS narrations   |
   |                                         | in Step-3 bank data           |
5  | Declared income vs total debt burden    | Step-1 monthly income vs      | Soft flag
   |                                         | total_emi + utility + rent    |
   |                                         | (full obligation scan)        |


===========================================================================
GLOBAL PROFILE UPDATE — STEP-9
===========================================================================

After all validations pass, the global profile is updated:

verified_profile.loans = [
  {
    "lender_name"          : "HDFC Bank",
    "emi_amount"           : 5000,
    "previous_debit_date"  : "2026-03-05",
    "latest_debit_date"    : "2026-04-05",
    "emi_bank_verified"    : true,
    "emi_recurring"        : true,
    "loan_status"          : "Active",
    "outstanding_balance"  : 85000
  },
  {
    "lender_name"          : "Bajaj Finserv",
    "emi_amount"           : 3200,
    "previous_debit_date"  : "2026-03-07",
    "latest_debit_date"    : "2026-04-07",
    "emi_bank_verified"    : true,
    "emi_recurring"        : true,
    "loan_status"          : "Active",
    "outstanding_balance"  : 42000
  }
]

verified_profile.debt_summary = {
  "total_monthly_emi"    : 8200,
  "emi_burden_ratio"     : 0.37,
  "active_loan_count"    : 2,
  "undisclosed_emi_flag" : false,
  "high_emi_burden"      : false
}


===========================================================================
ML FEATURES PRODUCED — P3 DEBT MANAGEMENT
===========================================================================

Feature                     | Description                              | Pillar
----------------------------|------------------------------------------|---------
emi_burden_ratio            | Total declared EMI ÷ monthly income      | P3
active_loan_count           | Number of declared active loans (0–6)    | P3
emi_bank_verified_binary    | At least 1 EMI confirmed in bank (0/1)   | P3
emi_recurring_binary        | Both prev + latest debit found (0/1)     | P3
undisclosed_emi_flag        | Hidden EMI detected from Step-3 (0/1)    | P3
emi_on_time_ratio           | On-time EMI payments ÷ total observed    | P3
                            | months (from Step-3 transaction history) |
debt_to_income_ratio        | Total outstanding balance ÷ annual income| P3
                            | (if API verified)                        |
high_emi_burden_binary      | emi_burden_ratio > 0.80 (0/1)            | P3

Total features produced: 8
Pillar impacted: P3 (Debt Management)


===========================================================================
TECHNICAL FLOW — STEP-9
===========================================================================

User opens Step-9
  ↓
Score impact message displayed
  ↓
GATE: "No Active Loans" toggle check
  User selects "I have no active loans"
    → All loan fields hidden
    → declared_no_loans = true
    → System silently scans Step-3 for undisclosed EMI patterns
    → If undisclosed EMIs found → undisclosed_emi_flag = true (soft)
    → Step-9 COMPLETED immediately
  ↓
[IF user declares loans]

  User enters Loan 1:
    → Lender Name
    → Monthly EMI Amount
    → Previous Debit Date
    → Latest Debit Date
    → Individual validation: all 4 fields + frequency sanity check
    ↓
  [OPTIONAL] User taps Verify button:
    → POST /gov/loan/verify
    → API response: loan_status, outstanding_balance, loan_type
    → Post-API validation: Active / Closed / Defaulted / Not Found
  ↓
  Bank cross-check (automatic):
    → Match Latest Debit Date in Step-3 canonical CSV (± 5%, ± 5 days)
    → Match Previous Debit Date in Step-3 canonical CSV
    → emi_bank_verified and emi_recurring flags set
  ↓
  [IF user taps "Add Another Loan"]
    → Same 5-field card appears
    → Same individual validation + optional API + bank cross-check
    → Repeat up to 6 loans total
  ↓
  Cross-internal validation:
    → Duplicate lender check (HARD FAIL if duplicate)
    → Total EMI burden vs Step-1 income (soft flag if > 80%)
    → EMI-to-income ratio computed (emi_burden_ratio)
  ↓
  Global validation:
    → Auto-detect EMI patterns from Step-3 bank data
    → Compare against declared loans
    → undisclosed_emi_flag if mismatch found (soft, non-blocking)
    → Full obligation scan: EMI + utility + rent vs declared income
  ↓
Update verified_profile.loans and verified_profile.debt_summary
  ↓
Step-9 Status = COMPLETED
  ↓
ALL 9 STEPS COMPLETE
  ↓
Meta-Learner (Logistic Regression) runs on all 89 features
  ↓
Final Credit Score computed (range: 300–900)
  ↓
Credit Report and Score Card generated for user


===========================================================================
OUTPUT OF STEP-9
===========================================================================

Step-9 Status           : COMPLETED
verified_profile update : loans array and debt_summary updated
P3 features produced    : 8 Debt Management features
Final trigger           : Meta-Learner → Credit Score (300–900)

===========================================================================
END OF STEP-9 SPECIFICATION
===========================================================================

--- specification folders_new\Inputs\step- wise - inputs\GigCredit — Complete Input Specif.txt ---
# GigCredit — Complete Input Specification (Steps 1–9)
**Version:** 2.1 — Production Planning Reference
**Status:** Final — All Steps Locked (Inputs Only)
**Scope:** All user-facing fields, toggles, uploads, and mandatory rules for every onboarding step

***

## Overview

GigCredit is a gig-worker credit scoring platform that collects financial, identity, and behavioural data across 9 onboarding steps. This README is the single reference document for all input fields across all steps.

### System Dimensions

| Dimension | Detail |
|---|---|
| Total Onboarding Steps | 9 |
| Steps with Mandatory Content | Steps 1, 2, 3, 4 (partial), 5 |
| Steps with Conditional Content | Step 4 (rent), Step 7 (vehicle insurance) |
| Steps with Optional-Only Content | Steps 6, 7 (mostly), 8, 9 |
| Dynamic Steps | Step 5 (fully reconfigures by work type) |
| Supported Work Types | Platform Worker, Vendor/Seller, Skilled Tradesperson, Freelancer |

### Input Count Summary

| Step | Section | Mandatory | Optional | Total |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Basic Profile | 11 | 1 | 12 |
| 2 | Identity / KYC | 6 | 0 | 6 |
| 3 | Bank Verification | 6 | 11 | 17 |
| 4 | Utility Bills | 18 | up to 13 | 31 |
| 5A | Platform Worker | 8 | 2 | 10 |
| 5B | Vendor / Seller | 3 | 4 | 7 |
| 5C | Skilled Tradesperson | 3 | 3 | 6 |
| 5D | Freelancer | 2 | 7 | 9 |
| 6 | Government Schemes | 0 (all conditional) | 10 | 10 |
| 7 | Insurance Coverage | 0–1 (conditional) | 2–3 | 3 |
| 8 | ITR and GST | 0 (all conditional) | up to 12 | 12 |
| 9 | EMI / Loan Behaviour | 1 toggle + 4/entry | 1/entry | up to 26 |

### Global Input Rules

- **Required** — must be provided before step completion.
- **Optional** — can be skipped without blocking the step.
- **Conditional Required** — becomes required only when a specific module or toggle is selected.
- Selecting a module and leaving it incomplete **blocks step completion**.
- All document uploads accept JPG, PNG, or PDF unless otherwise noted.

***

## Step 1 — Basic Profile

**Purpose:** Collect core demographic and professional details. The Work Type selected here configures all of Step 5 and downstream ML feature engineering. Vehicle Ownership controls Step 7 vehicle insurance mandatory status.

**UI:** Multi-section scrollable form. No document uploads.

### Section A — Personal Details

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Example | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Full Name | Single-line text | Alphabets + spaces | Ravi Kumar | ✅ Required |
| 2 | Date of Birth | Date picker (DD/MM/YYYY) | Calendar selector | 12/06/1997 | ✅ Required |
| 3 | Mobile Number | Numeric text field | 10-digit Indian mobile | 9876543210 | ✅ Required |
| 4 | Current Address | Multi-line text | Free text | No 14, Anna Nagar, Chennai | ✅ Required |
| 5 | Permanent Address | Multi-line text | Free text | Madurai, Tamil Nadu | ✅ Required |
| 6 | State of Residence | Dropdown | 28 states + 8 UTs | Tamil Nadu | ✅ Required |

### Section B — Professional Details

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Example | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 7 | Work Type | 4-card illustrated selector | One of four work types | Platform Worker | ✅ Required |
| 8 | Self-Declared Monthly Income (₹) | Numeric field with ₹ prefix | Positive integer | ₹22,000 | ✅ Required |
| 9 | Years in Current Profession | Numeric stepper | Integer 0–40 | 3 | ✅ Required |
| 10 | Number of Dependents | Numeric stepper | Integer 0–10 | 2 | ✅ Required |
| 11 | Vehicle Ownership | Radio button Yes / No | Boolean | Yes | ✅ Required |
| 12 | Secondary Income Source | Text + ₹ amount field | Free text + integer | Part-time delivery, ₹5,000 | ⬜ Optional |

### Work Type Cards (4 options)

| Card | Label | Example Occupations |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Platform Worker | Ola, Uber, Swiggy, Zomato, Blinkit, Rapido, Porter |
| 2 | Vendor / Seller | Fruit vendor, street food, kirana, small goods |
| 3 | Skilled Tradesperson | Electrician, plumber, carpenter, mason, tailor |
| 4 | Freelancer | Designer, developer, writer, consultant |

**Step 1 Total: 12 fields | Mandatory: 11 | Optional: 1**

## Step 2 — Identity Verification (KYC)

**Purpose:** Verify government-issued identity documents and confirm the person physically present matches the identity on file. Produces KYC verification status used in P5 identity scoring.

**UI:** Three upload cards with guide overlays. Selfie uses camera-only capture (no gallery).

### Aadhaar Card

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Aadhaar Number | Numeric field (4-4-4 segmented) | 12-digit number | ✅ Required |
| 2 | Aadhaar Card — Front Side | Image upload | JPG or PNG | ✅ Required |
| 3 | Aadhaar Card — Back Side | Image upload | JPG or PNG | ✅ Required |

> Both sides required: Front → name, DOB, Aadhaar number, photo. Back → full address for state and address cross-checks.

### PAN Card

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4 | PAN Number | Alphanumeric text field | 10-char (ABCDE1234F format) | ✅ Required |
| 5 | PAN Card Photo | Image upload | JPG or PNG | ✅ Required |

### Live Selfie

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 6 | Live Selfie | In-app camera trigger | Camera capture only (no gallery) | ✅ Required |

> Camera-only enforces physical presence. Selfie is compared against Aadhaar front photo using on-device face matching (MobileFaceNet).

**Step 2 Total: 6 fields | Mandatory: 6 | Optional: 0**

***

**UI:** Primary bank section visible by default. Secondary bank section collapsed behind a toggle. UPI section below both.

| 5 | Account Number | Numeric text | 9–18 digits | 1234567890 | ✅ Required |
| 6 | Bank Statement Upload | PDF file picker | PDF only, 6–12 months | — | ✅ Required |

## Step 7 — Insurance Coverage

**Purpose:** Capture insurance policy details as evidence of financial resilience and risk management. Feeds the P6 Financial Resilience scoring pillar.

**UI:** Three vertically stacked insurance cards. Each has a toggle. Vehicle Insurance becomes mandatory if Step 1 Vehicle Ownership = Yes.

### Module 1 — Health Insurance

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Health Insurance Selected? | Toggle Yes / No | Boolean | Optional |
| 2 | Health Policy Number | Single-line text | Alphanumeric | Conditional Required |
| 3 | Policy Holder Name | Single-line text | Plain text | Conditional Required |
| 4 | Health Policy Document Upload | File upload | JPG/PNG/PDF | Conditional Required |

### Module 2 — Vehicle Insurance

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 5 | Vehicle Insurance Selected? | Toggle Yes / No | Boolean | Required if Step-1 vehicle = Yes |
| 6 | Vehicle Policy Number | Single-line text | Alphanumeric | Conditional Required |
| 7 | Policy Holder Name | Single-line text | Plain text | Conditional Required |
| 8 | Vehicle Insurance Document Upload | File upload | JPG/PNG/PDF | Conditional Required |

> Dynamic rule: If Step 1 Vehicle Ownership = Yes → module must be completed. If No → optional or hidden.

### Module 3 — Life Insurance

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 9 | Life Insurance Selected? | Toggle Yes / No | Boolean | Optional |
| 10 | Life Policy Number | Single-line text | Alphanumeric | Conditional Required |
| 11 | Policy Holder Name | Single-line text | Plain text | Conditional Required |
| 12 | Life Policy Document Upload | File upload | JPG/PNG/PDF | Conditional Required |

**Step 7 Total: 12 fields (3 toggles + 9 detail fields) | Vehicle Insurance conditionally mandatory | Rest optional**

***

## Step 8 — ITR and GST Records

**Purpose:** Collect formal income declaration and tax compliance records. Strongest income signals in the system — improve confidence scores across P1, P4, P6, and the meta-learner.

**UI:** Two cards (ITR on top, GST below). Both collapsed by default. GST card expanded by default for Vendor and Freelancer work types.

> Step-level message: *"ITR and GST records are the strongest proof of income. Submitting them can improve your score by up to 22 points across multiple pillars."*

### Module 1 — Income Tax Return (ITR)

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | ITR Selected? | Toggle Yes / No | Boolean | Optional |
| 2 | PAN Number (as per ITR) | Single-line text | 10-char PAN (ABCDE1234F) | Conditional Required |
| 3 | Name as per ITR | Single-line text | Plain text | Conditional Required |
| 4 | Assessment Year | Dropdown | e.g. 2023-24, 2024-25 | Conditional Required |
| 5 | Annual Income in INR | Numeric text | Integer (Total Income field on ITR-V) | Conditional Required |
| 6 | ITR Acknowledgement Upload | File upload | JPG/PNG/PDF | Conditional Required |
| 7 | Form 26AS Upload | File upload | PDF only | ⬜ Optional |

### Module 2 — GST Records

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 8 | GST Selected? | Toggle Yes / No | Boolean | Optional |
| 9 | GSTIN | Single-line text uppercase | 15-char alphanumeric | Conditional Required |
| 10 | Legal Name as per GST | Single-line text | Plain text (as on GST RC) | Conditional Required |
| 11 | Annual Turnover in INR | Numeric text | Integer (from GSTR-3B totals) | Conditional Required |
| 12 | GST Document Upload | Multi-file upload | PDF (GSTR-3B returns + GST RC) | Conditional Required |

> GST document upload accepts: Multiple GSTR-3B return PDFs (up to 6 months) + GST Registration Certificate (REG-06).

### GST Visibility by Work Type

| Work Type | GST Section Behaviour |
|---|---|
| Vendor / Seller | Expanded by default, visually prominent |
| Freelancer | Expanded by default with income tip |
| Platform Worker | Collapsed, expandable on demand |
| Skilled Tradesperson | Collapsed, expandable on demand |

**Step 8 Total: 12 fields (2 toggles + 10 detail fields) | All Conditional Required when selected**

***

## Step 9 — EMI and Loan Behaviour

**Purpose:** Capture existing loan and EMI obligations to assess monthly debt repayment burden, regularity, and debt-to-income ratio signals. Feeds the P3 Debt Management scoring pillar. Cross-referenced against Step 3 bank statement debit entries.

**UI:** Top-level toggle "Do you have active EMIs?" If Yes, a repeatable loan entry card appears. Up to 5 loan entries allowed.

### Top-Level Toggle

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Active Loans / EMIs? | Toggle Yes / No | Boolean | Required |

> If No → Step 9 marked complete as "No active loans." No further input needed.

### Loan / EMI Entry Card (Repeatable — up to 5)

Each loan the user declares fills one card. Fields below apply per card:

| # | Field Name | UI Element | Input Format | Mandatory |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | Lender Name | Dropdown + free text | Bank / NBFC / MFI / Other | Required per entry |
| 3 | Monthly EMI Amount (₹) | Numeric text | Positive integer in INR | Required per entry |
| 4 | Previous Debit Date | Date picker | DD/MM/YYYY | Required per entry |
| 5 | Latest Debit Date | Date picker | DD/MM/YYYY | Required per entry |
| 6 | Loan Verification Trigger | Button (action) | Triggers bank cross-check | ⬜ Optional per entry |

> **Field 4 (Previous Debit Date)** must be earlier than Field 5 (Latest Debit Date).
> **Field 5 (Latest Debit Date)** must be ≤ today's date.
> **Field 6 (Loan Verification)** cross-references declared EMI against Step 3 bank statement debits. Non-blocking.

### Lender Dropdown Options

| Category | Examples |
|---|---|
| Public Banks | SBI, Canara, PNB, Bank of Baroda, Indian Bank |
| Private Banks | HDFC, ICICI, Axis, Kotak, IndusInd |
| NBFCs | Bajaj Finance, IIFL, Muthoot Finance, Shriram |
| MFIs | Ujjivan, Jana Bank, Spandana, ESAF |
| Other | Free-text entry |

### Multi-Entry Rules

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Minimum entries (if Yes) | 1 loan entry must be completed |
| Maximum entries | 5 loan entries per user |
| Add entry trigger | "Add Another Loan" button below each completed card |
| Remove entry trigger | Trash icon on each card |
| Validation on Continue | All Required fields (2–5) filled in every card |

**Step 9 Total: 1 toggle + up to 5 × 5 fields = 26 maximum | Mandatory: 4 fields per entry + 1 top toggle**

***

## Cross-Step Dependencies

| Trigger Field | Step | Affects |
|---|---|---|
| Work Type | Step 1 | Entire Step 5 layout (5A / 5B / 5C / 5D) |
| Work Type = Vendor / Freelancer | Step 1 | GST section shown expanded by default in Step 8 |
| Vehicle Ownership = Yes | Step 1 | Vehicle Insurance becomes mandatory in Step 7 |
| State of Residence | Step 1 | State median income anchor for all bank features |
| PAN Number | Step 2 | Cross-checked in Step 8 ITR module |
| Bank Statement Debits | Step 3 | Cross-checked against Step 4 bills, Step 6 scheme contributions, Step 7 premiums, Step 9 EMIs |
| Aadhaar Name | Step 2 | Cross-checked in Steps 5A (DL, platform name), 5B (SVANidhi, trade licence), 5C (skill cert), 8 (ITR name) |
| Vehicle Number (RC Book) | Step 5A | Cross-checked against Step 7 vehicle insurance policy |
| Rental Address | Step 4 (Module 6) | Cross-checked against Step 1 current address |

***

## Scoring Pillar Mapping

| Step | Scoring Pillars Fed |
|---|---|
| Step 1 | P1 Income Stability, P5 Identity, Meta-Learner work type selector |
| Step 2 | P5 Identity and KYC |
| Step 3 | P1 Income Stability, P2 Payment Discipline, P3 Debt Management, P4 Savings Behaviour |
| Step 4 | P2 Payment Discipline |
| Step 5 | P1 Income Stability, P5 Work Identity |
| Step 6 | P7 Social Accountability |
| Step 7 | P6 Financial Resilience |
| Step 8 | P1, P4, P6 (via declared income and tax compliance) |
| Step 9 | P3 Debt Management |

STEP-1-BASIC-PROFILE-INPUTS.txt

GIGCREDIT — STEP 1 BASIC PROFILE INPUT SPECIFICATION
Version 1.0
Status: Finalized
Scope: Input collection only
Note: This file covers only Step 1 user inputs, UI structure, field details, and mandatory/optional status. It does not include verification logic, validation logic, scoring features, or downstream rules.

============================================================
STEP 1 — BASIC PROFILE INFORMATION
============================================================

PURPOSE
Collect the user’s core personal and professional profile details at the start of onboarding. This step establishes the baseline identity, residency, and work profile information required before document-based steps begin. This is a form-only step and does not include any uploads.

------------------------------------------------------------
HOW THIS STEP IS PRESENTED
------------------------------------------------------------

Step 1 is shown as a scrollable form screen divided into two main sections:

1. Personal Details
2. Professional Details

The screen contains:
- Single-line text inputs
- Multi-line address fields
- Date picker
- Dropdown selector
- Numeric inputs / steppers
- Radio buttons
- A 4-option work type selector displayed as illustrated cards in a 2 × 2 layout

At the bottom of the screen, a Continue button is shown after all required Step 1 fields are filled.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION A — PERSONAL DETAILS
------------------------------------------------------------

Field 1
Field Name: Full Name
UI Element: Single-line text field
Input Type: Plain text
Example: Ravi Kumar
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the full name of the applicant as entered by the user.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 2
Field Name: Date of Birth
UI Element: Date picker
Input Type: Date
Display Format: DD/MM/YYYY
Example: 12/06/1997
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the applicant’s date of birth using a calendar-style date selector.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 3
Field Name: Mobile Number
UI Element: Numeric text field
Input Type: 10-digit mobile number
Example: 9876543210
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the primary mobile number used by the applicant.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 4
Field Name: Current Address
UI Element: Multi-line text field
Input Type: Free text address
Example: No 14, Anna Nagar, Chennai
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the applicant’s current residential address.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 5
Field Name: Permanent Address
UI Element: Multi-line text field
Input Type: Free text address
Example: Madurai, Tamil Nadu
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the applicant’s permanent residential address.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 6
Field Name: State of Residence
UI Element: Dropdown selector
Input Type: Single select
Options: All Indian States and Union Territories
Example: Tamil Nadu
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the state or union territory in which the applicant currently resides.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION B — PROFESSIONAL DETAILS
------------------------------------------------------------

Field 7
Field Name: Work Type
UI Element: Illustrated card selector
Input Type: Single choice
Mandatory: Yes

Available Options:

Option 1
Label: Platform Worker
Suggested Examples Shown:
- Ola
- Uber
- Swiggy
- Zomato
- Dunzo
- Porter
- Blinkit
- Rapido

Option 2
Label: Vendor / Seller
Suggested Examples Shown:
- Fruit seller
- Vegetable vendor
- Street food seller
- Kirana shop
- Small goods seller

Option 3
Label: Skilled Tradesperson
Suggested Examples Shown:
- Electrician
- Plumber
- Carpenter
- Welder
- Mason
- Tailor
- Mechanic

Option 4
Label: Freelancer
Suggested Examples Shown:
- Designer
- Developer
- Writer
- Content creator
- Consultant

Description:
The user selects exactly one work category from the four available options.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 8
Field Name: Self-Declared Monthly Income
UI Element: Numeric text field with currency prefix
Input Type: Monthly income in INR
Example: ₹22,000
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the applicant’s self-declared average monthly income.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 9
Field Name: Years in Current Profession
UI Element: Numeric stepper or numeric text field
Input Type: Integer
Example: 3
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture how many years the applicant has been working in their current profession or occupation type.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 10
Field Name: Number of Dependents
UI Element: Numeric stepper
Input Type: Integer
Example: 2
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture the number of family members or dependents financially supported by the applicant.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 11
Field Name: Vehicle Ownership
UI Element: Radio button selector
Input Type: Yes / No
Example: Yes
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
Capture whether the applicant owns or uses a vehicle associated with their livelihood or household.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 12
Field Name: Secondary Income Source
UI Element: Single-line text field + numeric amount field
Input Type: Text + amount in INR
Example: Part-time delivery, ₹5,000
Mandatory: No

Description:
Capture any secondary or side income source declared by the applicant, along with the approximate monthly amount.

------------------------------------------------------------
STEP 1 INPUT SUMMARY
------------------------------------------------------------

Total Input Fields: 12
Mandatory Fields: 11
Optional Fields: 1
Document Uploads in This Step: None

------------------------------------------------------------
STEP 1 SCREEN STRUCTURE SUMMARY
------------------------------------------------------------

Recommended display order:

A. Personal Details
1. Full Name
2. Date of Birth
3. Mobile Number
4. Current Address
5. Permanent Address
6. State of Residence

B. Professional Details
7. Work Type
8. Self-Declared Monthly Income
9. Years in Current Profession
10. Number of Dependents
11. Vehicle Ownership
12. Secondary Income Source

------------------------------------------------------------
END OF STEP 1 SPECIFICATION

STEP-2-IDENTITY-KYC-INPUTS.txt

GIGCREDIT — STEP 2 IDENTITY VERIFICATION INPUT SPECIFICATION
Version 1.0
Status: Finalized
Scope: Input collection only
Note: This file covers only Step 2 user inputs, UI structure, field details,
mandatory/optional status, and OCR extraction fields. It does not include
verification logic, validation logic, scoring features, or downstream rules.

============================================================
STEP 2 — IDENTITY VERIFICATION (KYC)
============================================================

PURPOSE
Collect and capture the user's government-issued identity documents and a
live selfie to confirm the person's identity. This step is document-upload
based and uses on-device OCR to extract key fields from each document.

------------------------------------------------------------
HOW THIS STEP IS PRESENTED
------------------------------------------------------------

Step 2 is shown as a vertically scrolling screen with four clearly labelled
upload cards displayed in sequence:

1. Aadhaar Card — Front Side
2. Aadhaar Card — Back Side
3. PAN Card
4. Live Selfie

Each document card shows:
- Card title
- A guide overlay image showing the correct way to photograph the document
- Upload button (camera or gallery)
- A status indicator showing Pending / Uploaded / Processing

The Live Selfie card uses camera capture only.
No gallery upload is permitted for the selfie.

At the bottom of the screen a Continue button is shown only after all
four mandatory uploads are completed.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION A — AADHAAR CARD
------------------------------------------------------------

Field 1
Field Name: Aadhaar Number
UI Element: Numeric text field, segmented display 4-4-4
Input Type: 12-digit numeric
Example: 7494 2006 7990
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
User manually enters their 12-digit Aadhaar number before uploading
the Aadhaar card images.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 2
Field Name: Aadhaar Card — Front Side
UI Element: Image upload card with guide overlay
Input Type: Camera capture or gallery image
Accepted Formats: JPG, PNG
Example Reference: Card showing name, DOB, gender, photo, Aadhaar number
Mandatory: Yes

OCR Fields Extracted from Aadhaar Front:
- Full name (English)
- Full name (regional language if printed)
- Date of birth
- Gender
- Aadhaar number
- Photo (used for face match against selfie)
- Issue date (if printed)

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 3
Field Name: Aadhaar Card — Back Side
UI Element: Image upload card with guide overlay
Input Type: Camera capture or gallery image
Accepted Formats: JPG, PNG
Example Reference: Card showing full address, state, PIN code
Mandatory: Yes

OCR Fields Extracted from Aadhaar Back:
- Full address (English)
- District
- State
- PIN code
- Father name or S/O name (if printed)

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION B — PAN CARD
------------------------------------------------------------

Field 4
Field Name: PAN Number
UI Element: Alphanumeric text field, uppercase
Input Type: 10-character alphanumeric
Format: ABCDE1234F
Example: IPZPP3254R
Mandatory: Yes

Description:
User manually enters their 10-character PAN number before uploading
the PAN card image.

------------------------------------------------------------

Field 5
Field Name: PAN Card Photo
UI Element: Image upload card with guide overlay
Input Type: Camera capture or gallery image
Accepted Formats: JPG, PNG
Example Reference: Card showing name, father name, DOB, PAN number, photo
Mandatory: Yes

OCR Fields Extracted from PAN Card:
- Full name
- Father's name
- Date of birth
- PAN number
- Signature (presence detected, not read)

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION C — LIVE SELFIE
------------------------------------------------------------

Field 6
Field Name: Live Selfie
UI Element: In-app camera trigger button
Input Type: Camera capture only
Gallery upload: NOT permitted
Accepted Format: JPG (captured in-app)
Mandatory: Yes

Screen guidance shown to user:
"Position your face inside the oval frame.
Make sure your face is well lit and clearly visible.
Remove glasses or cap if possible."

What is captured:
- Single front-facing photo of the applicant
- Used for on-device face match against Aadhaar front photo

------------------------------------------------------------
STEP 2 INPUT SUMMARY
------------------------------------------------------------

Total Input Fields: 6
Mandatory Fields: 6
Optional Fields: 0
Document Uploads: 3 images (Aadhaar front, Aadhaar back, PAN card)
Live Captures: 1 selfie (camera only)

------------------------------------------------------------
STEP 2 SCREEN STRUCTURE SUMMARY
------------------------------------------------------------

Recommended display order:

A. Aadhaar Card
1. Aadhaar Number (manual entry)
2. Aadhaar Card Front Upload
3. Aadhaar Card Back Upload

B. PAN Card
4. PAN Number (manual entry)
5. PAN Card Photo Upload

C. Live Selfie
6. Selfie Capture (camera only)

------------------------------------------------------------
END OF STEP 2 SPECIFICATION
------------------------------------------------------------
--- specification folders_new\Inputs\step- wise - inputs\STEP-3 — BANK VERIFICATION.txt ---
STEP-3 — BANK VERIFICATION
GigCredit Onboarding Specification
Version: Final Input Structure
Status: Locked for UI/Input Definition

--------------------------------------------------
1. PURPOSE
--------------------------------------------------

Step-3 collects the user's bank account details and transaction history to evaluate income patterns, expense behaviour, EMI obligations, savings habits, and debt indicators.

This is the most data-rich step in the GigCredit onboarding system and produces the majority of financial features used across P1 through P4 scoring.

This document defines:
- how Step-3 is shown on screen,
- what inputs are collected,
- which fields are mandatory or optional,
- what gets extracted after submission,
- and how bank and UPI transaction data are structured.

This document does NOT define the validation and verification layers yet.
Those will be discussed separately.

--------------------------------------------------
2. SCREEN PRESENTATION
--------------------------------------------------

Step-3 is shown as a three-part screen:

Section A — Primary Bank Account
Section B — Secondary Bank Account
Section C — UPI Statement

The primary bank section is shown by default.

The secondary bank section is collapsed by default and shown only when the user enables a toggle.

The UPI section appears below the bank sections and remains optional.

--------------------------------------------------
3. SECTION A — PRIMARY BANK ACCOUNT
--------------------------------------------------

This section is mandatory.

The following fields are collected from the user:

1. Bank Name
   - UI Element: Searchable dropdown
   - Format: Indian banks list
   - Example: HDFC Bank
   - Mandatory: Yes

2. Account Holder Name
   - UI Element: Single-line text field
   - Format: Plain text
   - Example: Ravi Kumar
   - Mandatory: Yes

3. Bank Branch Name
   - UI Element: Single-line text field
   - Format: Plain text
   - Example: Anna Nagar Chennai
   - Mandatory: Yes

4. IFSC Code
   - UI Element: Alphanumeric text field
   - Format: Uppercase, 11 characters
   - Example: HDFC0001234
   - Mandatory: Yes

5. Account Number
   - UI Element: Numeric text field
   - Format: 9–18 digits
   - Example: 1234567890
   - Mandatory: Yes

6. Bank Statement
   - UI Element: PDF file picker
   - Format: PDF only
   - Coverage Required: Minimum 6 months, maximum 12 months
   - Mandatory: Yes

7. MICR Code
   - UI Element: Numeric text field
   - Format: 9-digit number
   - Example: 600240012
   - Mandatory: No

--------------------------------------------------
4. SECTION B — SECONDARY BANK ACCOUNT
--------------------------------------------------

This section is optional and collapsed by default.

It is shown behind a toggle labelled:

"Add Secondary Bank Account (recommended for platform workers)"

UI tip shown near the toggle:

"Platform workers often receive gig income in a separate account. Adding it gives a more accurate picture of your total earnings and can significantly improve your score."

If the user enables the secondary account toggle, the following fields become visible.

8. Enable Secondary Account
   - UI Element: Toggle switch
   - Format: Boolean ON/OFF
   - Mandatory: Optional

9. Bank Name (secondary)
   - UI Element: Same as primary
   - Format: Same as primary
   - Mandatory: Conditional

10. Account Holder Name (secondary)
    - UI Element: Same as primary
    - Format: Same as primary
    - Mandatory: Conditional

11. Branch Name (secondary)
    - UI Element: Same as primary
    - Format: Same as primary
    - Mandatory: Conditional

12. IFSC Code (secondary)
    - UI Element: Same as primary
    - Format: Same as primary
    - Mandatory: Conditional

13. Account Number (secondary)
    - UI Element: Same as primary
    - Format: Same as primary
    - Mandatory: Conditional

14. Bank Statement (secondary)
    - UI Element: PDF file picker
    - Format: PDF
    - Coverage Required: Minimum 6 months
    - Mandatory: Conditional

15. MICR Code (secondary)
    - UI Element: Same as primary
    - Format: 9-digit number
    - Mandatory: Optional

Conditional means:
These fields become required only if the user enables the secondary account toggle.

--------------------------------------------------
5. SECTION C — UPI STATEMENT
--------------------------------------------------

This section is optional.

The following fields are collected:

16. UPI Platform
    - UI Element: Dropdown selector
    - Format: PhonePe / Google Pay / Paytm / BHIM / Other
    - Mandatory: No

17. UPI Statement
    - UI Element: PDF file picker
    - Format: PDF export from UPI app
    - Mandatory: No

--------------------------------------------------
6. STEP-3 INPUT SUMMARY
--------------------------------------------------

Total Inputs: 17

Primary Mandatory Inputs:
- Bank Name
- Account Holder Name
- Bank Branch Name
- IFSC Code
- Account Number
- Bank Statement

Optional Inputs:
- MICR Code
- Entire secondary bank section
- Entire UPI section

Secondary bank fields are conditional and become required only when the secondary account toggle is enabled.

--------------------------------------------------
7. WHAT GETS EXTRACTED AFTER SUBMISSION
--------------------------------------------------

Step-3 produces structured extraction outputs from the uploaded bank statement and optional UPI statement.

--------------------------------------------------
7A. FROM BANK STATEMENT (PDF PARSER)
--------------------------------------------------

Every transaction is extracted with the following fields:

1. Date
   Description:
   Transaction date

2. Amount
   Description:
   Credit or debit value in rupees

3. Description / Narration
   Description:
   Raw transaction text from bank statement

4. Balance
   Description:
   Closing balance after transaction

5. Transaction Mode
   Description:
   UPI / NEFT / IMPS / ATM / CASH / CHEQUE

This transaction data becomes the core financial timeline used in downstream feature engineering.

--------------------------------------------------
7B. TRANSACTION CATEGORY TAGGING
--------------------------------------------------

Each extracted transaction is automatically tagged into one of the following categories:

1. INCOME_SALARY
   Meaning:
   Regular salary credit

2. INCOME_GIG
   Meaning:
   Platform or gig payout credit

3. INCOME_UPI
   Meaning:
   UPI incoming transfer

4. INCOME_CASH
   Meaning:
   Cash deposit

5. EMI
   Meaning:
   Loan repayment debit

6. UTILITY
   Meaning:
   Electricity, gas, or mobile bill debit

7. RENT
   Meaning:
   Rent payment debit

8. FOOD
   Meaning:
   Food or restaurant debit

9. FUEL
   Meaning:
   Fuel-related debit

10. ATM
    Meaning:
    ATM withdrawal

11. TRANSFER
    Meaning:
    Outgoing transfer

12. OTHER
    Meaning:
    Unclassified transaction

--------------------------------------------------
7C. SECONDARY BANK MERGE RULE
--------------------------------------------------

If the user provides both primary and secondary bank statements:

- both transaction sets are merged into one unified timeline,
- duplicate transactions are flagged and removed.

Duplicate removal rule:
- same date plus or minus 1 day,
- and same amount plus or minus 5 rupees,
- then the transaction is treated as a duplicate candidate and removed from the merged output.

--------------------------------------------------
7D. FROM UPI STATEMENT
--------------------------------------------------

If the user uploads a UPI statement, the system extracts UPI transaction records and uses them to cross-validate UPI credits against bank statement credits.

This UPI upload acts as an additional supporting financial source and helps confirm transaction consistency across platforms.

--------------------------------------------------
8. STEP-3 OUTPUT ROLE IN GIGCREDIT
--------------------------------------------------

The data collected and extracted from Step-3 is later used to generate financial features related to:

- income behaviour,
- payment discipline,
- debt burden,
- expense patterns,
- savings habits,
- and transaction consistency.

Step-3 is therefore a major input source for the scoring pillars P1 through P4.

--------------------------------------------------
9. IMPORTANT BOUNDARY FOR THIS DOCUMENT
--------------------------------------------------

This Step-3 specification includes only:

- screen structure,
- user inputs,
- field requirements,
- extraction outputs,
- category tagging,
- and merge behaviour.

This document intentionally excludes:

- individual validation rules,
- cross-internal validation rules,
- cross-step validation rules,
- API verification logic,
- OCR confidence rules,
- parser error handling,
- fraud checks,
- and acceptance/rejection logic.

Those validation and verification layers will be defined separately in the next specification.

--------------------------------------------------
10. FINAL LOCK
--------------------------------------------------

This Step-3 input structure is final.

Do not add new inputs.
Do not remove existing inputs.
Do not rename the locked user-facing fields unless explicitly revised in a later version.

End of Step-3 Specification.

STEP-4 — UTILITY BILLS AND RECURRING PAYMENTS
GigCredit Onboarding Specification
Version: Updated — Rent Bill Added
Status: Locked — Inputs Only (No Verification Logic)

--------------------------------------------------
1. PURPOSE
--------------------------------------------------

Step-4 captures recurring payment bills to establish:
  - Address continuity proof
  - Regular payment behaviour
  - Financial obligation consistency

Supported bill types:
  1. Electricity Bill
  2. Water Bill
  3. Gas / LPG Bill
  4. Mobile / Phone Bill      ← existing
  5. Internet / Broadband Bill
  6. Rent Receipt / Agreement ← NEW ADDITION

All modules are optional globally.
If a module is selected, its fields become required.

--------------------------------------------------
2. HOW THIS STEP IS PRESENTED
--------------------------------------------------

Six bill cards displayed vertically.
Each card shows:
  - Bill type and brief description
  - Score impact hint
  - Toggle: "I have this bill" (Yes / No)
  - When Yes → expand fields and upload

--------------------------------------------------
3. MODULE 1 — ELECTRICITY BILL
--------------------------------------------------

Section A — Selection

#  | Field Name                  | UI Element       | Input Format   | Mandatory
---|-----------------------------|------------------|----------------|----------
1  | Electricity Bill Selected?  | Toggle Yes / No  | Boolean        | Optional

Section B — Bill Details
(Visible only when Selected? = Yes)

#  | Field Name                  | UI Element         | Input Format          | Mandatory
---|-----------------------------|--------------------|-----------------------|----------
2  | Consumer Number             | Single-line text   | Alphanumeric          | Conditional Required
3  | Name as per Bill            | Single-line text   | Plain text            | Conditional Required

--------------------------------------------------
8. MODULE 6 — RENT RECEIPT / AGREEMENT ← NEW
--------------------------------------------------

Real-world mapping:
In India, rent proof exists in two forms:
  a) Rent Receipt — issued by landlord monthly,
     contains landlord name, tenant name, address,
     rent amount, and month.
  b) Rent Agreement (Leave & License) — a legal
     document containing tenant name, landlord
     name, property address, monthly rent amount,
     and agreement period (start/end date).

Both are widely used as address and payment proofs.
These fields exist on real documents, so they are
valid explicit inputs.

Section A — Selection

#  | Field Name               | UI Element       | Input Format   | Mandatory
---|--------------------------|------------------|----------------|----------
32 | Rent Proof Selected?     | Toggle Yes / No  | Boolean        | Optional

Section B — Rent Details
(Visible only when Selected? = Yes)

#  | Field Name               | UI Element          | Input Format               | Mandatory
---|--------------------------|---------------------|----------------------------|----------
33 | Tenant Name              | Single-line text    | Plain text                 | Conditional Required
34 | Landlord Name            | Single-line text    | Plain text                 | Conditional Required
35 | Rental Address           | Multi-line text     | Plain text (full address)  | Conditional Required
36 | Monthly Rent Amount (₹)  | Numeric field       | Positive integer           | Conditional Required
37 | Rent Period / Month      | Month-Year picker   | MM/YYYY                    | Conditional Required
38 | Rent Document Upload     | File upload         | JPG/PNG/PDF                | Conditional Required

Field notes:
- Field 33 (Tenant Name):
    Name of the person paying rent — must match
    Step-1 name for address proof purposes.
- Field 34 (Landlord Name):
    Name of property owner as printed on receipt
    or agreement.
- Field 35 (Rental Address):
    Full address of the rented property. Used for
    address continuity cross-check with Step-1
    current address.
- Field 36 (Monthly Rent Amount):
    Rent in ₹ as stated on the receipt/agreement.
    Cross-checked against bank statement debits
    from Step-3.
- Field 37 (Rent Period / Month):
    Month and year the receipt/agreement covers.
- Field 38 (Rent Document Upload):
    Accepted documents:
      • Monthly rent receipt (signed by landlord)
      • Registered or notarised rent agreement
      • Leave and License agreement PDF

Module             | Fields per Module | Mandatory Behaviour
-------------------|-------------------|---------------------
Electricity Bill   | 5                 | All Conditional Required when selected
Water Bill         | 5                 | All Conditional Required when selected
Gas / LPG Bill     | 5                 | All Conditional Required when selected

Internet Bill      | 5                 | All Conditional Required when selected
Rent Receipt       | 6                 | All Conditional Required when selected ← NEW

Total selection toggles : 6
Total fields (all modules selected) : 32 input fields
                                     + 6 toggles = 38

Global rules:
- All modules are OPTIONAL at step level.
- Selecting a module makes all its fields
  REQUIRED before that module is complete.
- User may select any combination of modules.
- At least ONE bill is recommended for address
  and payment continuity signals, but not
  enforced as a hard block.

--- specification folders_new\Inputs\step- wise - inputs\STEP-6 — GOVERNMENT SCHEMES AND SOC.txt ---
STEP-6 — GOVERNMENT SCHEMES AND SOCIAL SECURITY
GigCredit Onboarding Specification
Version: Final Input Structure
Status: Locked — Inputs Only (No Verification Logic)

--------------------------------------------------
1. PURPOSE
--------------------------------------------------

Step-6 captures the user’s participation in recognised
government schemes and social security programs.

All schemes in this step are OPTIONAL at the global level.

Per-scheme rule:
- If the user DOES NOT select a scheme → no inputs required.
- If the user selects a scheme → that scheme’s ID/reference
  field AND proof document become REQUIRED for that scheme.

Verification and validation logic are defined in a
separate Step-6 verification document.

--------------------------------------------------
2. HOW THIS STEP IS PRESENTED
--------------------------------------------------

The screen shows a vertical list of scheme modules.
Each module has:
  - A toggle: "I have this scheme" (Yes / No)
  - When Yes → expand and show ID + proof upload fields.
  - When No → module stays collapsed and ignored.

Supported schemes:
  1. PM SVANidhi (Street Vendor scheme)
  2. eShram (Unorganised Workers UAN)
  3. PM-SYM (Shram Yogi Maandhan Pension)
  4. PMJJBY (Life Insurance)
  5. PMMY / MUDRA Loan
  6. PPF (Public Provident Fund)
  7. Udyam / MSME Registration

All modules follow the same pattern:
  - Scheme Selected? (toggle)
  - Scheme ID / Reference Number
  - Scheme Document Upload

--------------------------------------------------
3. MODULE 1 — PM SVANIDHI (STREET VENDOR SCHEME)
--------------------------------------------------

Note:
Real PM SVANidhi approval letters and certificates
contain an Application ID or similar reference ID
linked to the vendor’s loan/scheme record.[file:20][file:33]

Section A — Selection

#  | Field Name                  | UI Element           | Input Format          | Mandatory
---|-----------------------------|----------------------|----------------------|----------
1  | PM SVANidhi Selected?       | Toggle Yes / No      | Boolean              | Optional

Section B — Scheme Details
(Visible only when Selected? = Yes)

#  | Field Name                  | UI Element           | Input Format          | Mandatory
---|-----------------------------|----------------------|----------------------|----------
2  | SVANidhi Reference / App ID | Single-line text     | Alphanumeric ID      | Conditional Required
3  | SVANidhi Proof Document     | File upload          | JPG/PNG/PDF          | Conditional Required

Field 2 justification:
  - The PM SVANidhi approval/recommendation letters
    include an Application ID / reference number which
    uniquely identifies the vendor’s scheme record.[file:20][file:33]

Accepted documents for Field 3:
  - PM SVANidhi approval letter
  - PM SVANidhi sanction letter
  - PM SVANidhi beneficiary certificate

--------------------------------------------------
4. MODULE 2 — ESHRAM REGISTRATION
--------------------------------------------------

Note:
The eShram card clearly prints a 12-digit
“Universal Account Number (UAN)” as the primary
identifier on the front of the card.[web:48][web:54]

---|-----------------------------|----------------------|----------------------|----------

Field 5 justification:
  - UAN is the official 12-digit ID assigned on
    registration and is printed as “Universal Account
    Number (UAN)” on the eShram card itself.[web:48][web:54]

--------------------------------------------------

STEP-7 — INSURANCE COVERAGE
GigCredit Onboarding Specification
Version: Final Input Structure
Status: Locked — Inputs Only (No Verification Logic)

--------------------------------------------------
1. PURPOSE
--------------------------------------------------

Step-7 captures the user’s insurance coverage as
evidence of financial resilience and risk management.

This document defines ONLY user-facing inputs:
  - Selection toggles
  - Text fields
  - File upload fields
  - Mandatory / Optional rules

All OCR, API checks, and validation logic are defined
in a separate Step-7 verification specification.

Supported insurance types:
  1. Health Insurance
  2. Vehicle (Motor) Insurance
  3. Life Insurance

Global rule:
- All three insurance types are OPTIONAL at the step level.
- Vehicle Insurance becomes CONDITIONALLY REQUIRED when
  Step-1 Vehicle Ownership = Yes.

--------------------------------------------------
2. HOW THIS STEP IS PRESENTED
--------------------------------------------------

The screen shows three vertically stacked insurance cards:
  - Health Insurance
  - Vehicle Insurance
  - Life Insurance

Each card contains:
  - A short description of what it covers
  - A score impact hint (UX only, not inputs)
  - A toggle: "I have this insurance" (Yes / No)
  - When Yes → expand: show Policy Number, Policy Holder
    Name, and Policy Document Upload fields.

Dynamic behaviour:
  - Health Insurance → always optional.
  - Life Insurance   → always optional.
  - Vehicle Insurance:
      • Required if Step-1 Vehicle Ownership = Yes.
      • Optional (or hidden) if Vehicle Ownership = No.[file:20][file:33]

--------------------------------------------------
3. MODULE 1 — HEALTH INSURANCE
--------------------------------------------------

Real-world documents:
Indian health insurance policy schedules and bonds
always contain:
  - Policy Number
  - Policy Holder Name
  - Insurer Name
  - Sum Insured
  - Policy Start/Expiry Dates.[file:33][web:63][web:69][web:72][web:75]

These are printed on the policy schedule or certificate,
so “Policy Number” and “Policy Holder Name” are valid
explicit input fields.

Section A — Selection

#  | Field Name                    | UI Element         | Input Format       | Mandatory
---|-------------------------------|--------------------|--------------------|----------
1  | Health Insurance Selected?    | Toggle Yes / No    | Boolean            | Optional

Section B — Policy Details
(Visible only when Selected? = Yes)

#  | Field Name                    | UI Element         | Input Format       | Mandatory
---|-------------------------------|--------------------|--------------------|----------
2  | Health Policy Number          | Single-line text   | Alphanumeric ID    | Conditional Required
3  | Policy Holder Name            | Single-line text   | Plain text         | Conditional Required
4  | Health Policy Document Upload | File upload        | JPG/PNG/PDF        | Conditional Required

Notes:
- Field 2 maps to the Policy Number printed on the
  health policy schedule/bond.[web:63][web:69][web:75]
- Field 3 maps to the insured / proposer name printed
  on the policy schedule.[web:63][web:69][web:75]
- Field 4 accepts:
    • Scanned policy schedule
    • E-policy PDF
    • Insurer-issued certificate
